Abstract: Inter-crystal scatter (ICS) events pose a significant challenge in ultrahigh- resolution positron emission tomography (UHR-PET), especially as detector crystals become smaller and their readouts increasingly segmented. Current approaches either reject these events, reducing sensitivity, or accept them with suboptimal positioning algorithms, degrading image resolution. We present a feed forward neural network to optimize ICS event recovery by inferring the line-of-response belonging to the first Compton interaction. Our approach was validated using both Monte Carlo simulations and experimental data from the fully pixelated LabPET-IIbased preclinical and brain UHR-PET scanners.Results demonstrate a 70% to 106% increase in sensitivity while preserving sub-millimeter spatial resolvability (down to 1.6 mm) compared to conventional methods. This ICS recovery approach is an effective solution that compensates for the lower detection efficiency of small, pixelated detectors in UHR-PET, enabling reduced scan times and lower radiation doses while largely preserving image quality.

</details>


### [137] [Capacity Confounds and Coverage Guarantees in Adaptive Sub-model Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07157)
*Alireza Moayedikia,Alicia Troncoso Lora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过HAS-FL框架研究子模型联邦学习中基于数据异质性自适应分配容量的可行性，发现容量效应混淆了异质性估计，自适应分配存在隐藏失败模式，参数覆盖而非分配智能才是保护精度的关键。

Research relevance: 1分：与本研究方向基本无关。本文聚焦联邦学习中的子模型容量分配问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的剪枝/量化/蒸馏等核心方法。虽然涉及"子模型训练"（sub-model training）和"容量缩减"（width-reduced），但这是联邦学习场景下的客户端资源约束问题，而非遥感在轨目标检测模型的轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分：与本研究方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。本文讨论的是联邦学习场景下的子模型容量分配问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化核心技术，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴价值极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：联邦学习中的子模型容量分配；应用场景：分布式客户端训练；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。摘要未说明具体遥感或目标检测任务。

Model architecture: HAS-FL框架，一种自适应容量分配框架。子模型（sub-model）是全局模型的宽度缩减版本。摘要未说明具体网络架构。

Lightweight method: 涉及子模型训练（sub-model training），即训练全局模型的宽度缩减版本以适配资源受限客户端。但这不是传统意义上的模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS），而是联邦学习中的容量分配策略。摘要未说明剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏或NAS等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等星上/边缘部署相关信息。虽然涉及资源受限客户端，但未给出具体部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 使用了多个数据集（multiple datasets），包括图像基准（image benchmarks）和自然划分的文本基准（naturally partitioned text benchmark）。摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 主要发现：(1) 更新散度估计的客户端异质性被容量效应主导而非数据；(2) 自适应分配存在隐藏失败模式——当所有客户端被限制在满宽度以下时，未覆盖的参数保持随机初始化并逐渐破坏全局模型；(3) 在相同平均预算下，随机分配与自适应分配在图像基准上表现无差异，在文本基准上自适应策略最弱但消耗最多容量。

Limitations: 本文明确指出自适应分配的优势来自容量预算和参数覆盖而非分配智能，未来设计需要能够与容量效应分离的异质性信号。与遥感在轨目标检测无关，缺乏对遥感场景、目标检测任务和轻量化部署的讨论。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。但以下思路可间接参考：(1) 子模型训练（sub-model training）的思路——在星上资源受限场景下，训练完整大模型不可行时，可考虑训练宽度缩减的子模型；(2) 参数覆盖（parameter coverage）的重要性——在模型轻量化时，确保关键参数不被丢弃比智能分配策略更重要；(3) 容量效应与数据异质性混淆的警示——在评估轻量化模型性能时需注意控制变量。但整体而言，本文与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化的直接关联度很低。

Abstract: Sub-model federated learning lets resource-constrained clients train width-reduced versions of a global model, but existing methods allocate capacity by device resources alone. A natural next step, allocating capacity by each client's data heterogeneity as estimated from the updates the server already observes, has been repeatedly suggested. We ask whether that step is possible, using HAS-FL, an adaptive capacity-allocation framework, as a test case. Our findings are threefold. First, validated against ground-truth label-distribution divergence on reproducible partitions, update-divergence estimates of client heterogeneity are dominated by capacity rather than data: across two corrected estimators, multiple datasets, and all seeds, the estimates correlate strongly and negatively with device capacity, and no data signal remains once capacity is controlled for. This previously undocumented confound affects any method estimating client statistics from sub-model updates. Second, adaptive allocation has a hidden failure mode: when every client is capped below full width, the uncovered parameters stay at random initialization and progressively corrupt the global model. A simple coverage guarantee removes the failure and explains why uniform allocation collapses. Third, a matched-budget control settles what adaptivity contributes: random allocation to the same average budget performs no differently on both image benchmarks, and on the naturally partitioned text benchmark the adaptive policy is the weakest of the three strategies while consuming the most capacity. Sub-model training remains valuable because it admits constrained clients at quadratically reduced cost, but what protects accuracy is parameter coverage rather than allocation intelligence. Its apparent benefits come from capacity budgeting and coverage, and future designs need heterogeneity signals separable from capacity effects.

</details>


### [138] [Edge Sparsification via Temporal Forman-Ricci Curvature for Dynamic Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07158)
*Poupak Azad,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Kiarash Shamsi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于网络曲率（Forman-Ricci curvature）的时序图边稀疏化框架 TRicci，通过保留预测性时序结构信息，在压缩约80%边的情况下将端到端训练与推理时间平均降低55.94%，且不显著损失预测性能。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文属于泛机器学习/图学习领域，聚焦 temporal graph learning（时序图学习）与 edge sparsification（边稀疏化），面向金融交易网络、通信系统、社交平台等动态交互系统。与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能均无直接关系；不涉及 object detection、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等遥感目标检测任务；也不涉及 pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT 等模型轻量化/部署手段。其"稀疏化降低计算开销"的思想与轻量化目标有间接类比价值，但场景与任务完全不同。

Reading priority: 2分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，属于泛图学习/时序图稀疏化方法。仅其"基于几何度量的稀疏化剪枝准则"和"端到端时间开销评估"思路有轻微借鉴价值，不建议作为核心参考文献，可作背景了解。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：时序图学习（temporal graph learning）中的图级预测任务（graph-level prediction）。应用场景：金融交易网络、通信系统、在线社交平台等动态交互系统。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感/视觉数据）。目标类别：不适用（非目标检测）。

Model architecture: 核心是 TRicci 框架，将经典 Forman-Ricci curvature 扩展到有向加权时序图（directed weighted temporal graphs），通过捕获 structural support（结构支撑）、temporal recency（时间近邻性）和 local interaction competition（局部交互竞争）来指导边稀疏化。摘要未说明具体下游图学习模型结构。

Lightweight method: 采用 edge sparsification（边稀疏化）作为计算加速手段，基于网络曲率（network curvature）作为剪枝准则，属于数据/图结构层面的压缩，而非模型参数层面的剪枝、量化、蒸馏或NAS。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等模型级轻量化指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关内容。仅报告端到端下游训练与推理时间平均降低55.94%，以及约80%的边稀疏化比例，但未给出具体延迟、参数量、FLOPs、显存或能耗数据，且场景为通用图学习而非在轨遥感推理。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：9个交易网络（transaction networks）和3个时序图基准数据集（temporal graph benchmark datasets）。评价指标：图级预测任务上的预测性能（predictive performance），具体指标名称摘要未说明；另报告稀疏化比例（约80%）和端到端训练/推理时间降低幅度（平均55.94%）。

Experiments: 在9个交易网络和3个时序图基准数据集上验证，TRicci 在多个图级预测任务上保持预测性能，同时将时序图稀疏化约80%，端到端下游训练和推理时间平均降低55.94%，且预测性能无显著下降。摘要未说明与具体基线方法的对比细节及消融实验。

Limitations: 摘要未说明：具体下游模型结构、评价指标名称、与基线方法的详细对比、消融实验、稀疏化对长尾/小样本结构的影响、稀疏化后图结构保真度分析。此外，该方法面向通用动态图系统，未涉及遥感图像/目标检测场景，其"曲率剪枝"思想能否迁移到遥感检测模型的特征/通道剪枝尚需验证。

Ideas for my research: 尽管场景不同，其核心思想对"轻量化"课题有间接借鉴：1) 用几何/拓扑度量（如曲率）作为剪枝准则，可类比用于遥感检测网络中以结构重要性为依据的通道/连接剪枝，替代单纯基于幅值或BN因子的剪枝；2) "在强压缩下保留预测性结构信息"的思路可迁移到遥感小目标检测中，指导在压缩模型时优先保留对小目标响应重要的特征通道；3) 其"端到端训练+推理时间"联合评估方式提示在轨部署评估应同时关注训练侧与推理侧开销。但需注意其面向图结构而非卷积特征，直接迁移需重新设计。

Abstract: Temporal graph learning has become essential for analyzing real-world systems whose interactions continuously evolve over time, including financial transaction networks, communication systems, and online social platforms. However, learning from large-scale temporal graphs remains computationally challenging when networks are dense and rapidly changing. To address this limitation, we propose a network-curvature-inspired edge sparsification framework for dynamic graph learning. Our proposed method, TRicci, extends classical Forman-Ricci curvature to directed weighted temporal graphs by capturing structural support, temporal recency, and local interaction competition.
  Experiments on 9 transaction networks and 3 temporal graph benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework preserves predictive performance across multiple graph-level prediction tasks. The results show that TRicci sparsifies temporal graphs by approximately 80% while reducing end-to-end downstream training and inference time by an average of 55.94%, without substantial degradation in predictive performance. Our findings suggest that temporal curvature can serve as a principled basis for scalable temporal graph learning by preserving predictive temporal-structural information under substantial sparsification.

</details>


### [139] [Fluid-DiT: Graph-Free Diffusion Transformers for Fluid Flow Simulations Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07161)
*Shentong Mo,Guolin Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Fluid-DiT，一种无图扩散Transformer，用于从非结构化网格中高效采样复杂流体流动的平衡分布，替代了图神经网络扩散方法。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测、轻量化完全无关，属于计算流体力学（CFD）领域的生成式建模方法，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：流体动力学模拟中的平衡分布采样；场景：层流圆柱尾流、椭圆流系统、湍流3D机翼实验；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Fluid-DiT：基于扩散Transformer（DiT）的无图架构，用注意力机制替代图消息传递，采用潜在空间公式解耦几何保真度与分布学习。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：层流圆柱尾流、椭圆流系统、湍流3D机翼实验（均为流体力学基准）；评价指标：R²相关系数、Wasserstein距离。

Experiments: 与基于图的扩散基线（如DGN）对比，Fluid-DiT在样本质量和分布精度上均更优，且能从短轨迹泛化到未见雷诺数和几何形状。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无任何可迁移的轻量化或检测方法；摘要未讨论计算开销、实时性、边缘部署等关键问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于流体力学生成建模，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩，对本课题无参考价值。

Abstract: Simulating complex fluid flows requires capturing full equilibrium distributions rather than just mean trajectories, yet high-fidelity solvers remain computationally prohibitive. Recent advances, such as Diffusion Graph Networks (DGNs), have combined diffusion models with graph neural networks to sample equilibrium states directly from unstructured meshes, enabling distributional accuracy even from short simulations. However, graph-based diffusion approaches suffer from hand-crafted architectural constraints, limited receptive fields in message passing, and costly multi-scale designs, which restrict scalability to larger and more complex domains. We propose Fluid-DiT, a Graph-Free Diffusion Transformer that replaces graph message passing with attention-based denoising, eliminating explicit graph design while preserving the ability to model distributions of chaotic flows. Our framework introduces a latent-space formulation that disentangles geometric fidelity from distributional learning, reducing high-frequency artifacts and accelerating sampling. By leveraging the transformer's global receptive field, Fluid-DiT naturally captures both local flow structures and long-range correlations without requiring hierarchical graph coarsening. On canonical benchmarks including laminar cylinder wakes, ellipse-flow systems, and turbulent 3D wing experiments, Fluid-DiT consistently outperforms graph-based diffusion baselines in both sample quality and distributional accuracy, achieving higher $R^2$ correlations and lower Wasserstein distances. Moreover, it generalizes robustly from short, incomplete trajectories to unseen Reynolds numbers and geometries, demonstrating strong scalability.

</details>


### [140] [Momba: Network Modernization Improves Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07180)
*Adam Štafa,Santeri Heiskanen,Petr Novotný,Joni Pajarinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探讨在深度强化学习（RL）和多目标强化学习（MORL）中引入更优的网络架构设计（观测/特征归一化、权重归一化、分布回报建模）来提升算法性能，不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于深度强化学习（RL）和多目标强化学习（MORL）中的网络架构改进，属于强化学习算法优化范畴，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测任务或任何模型压缩/加速技术。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多目标强化学习（MORL）策略优化；应用场景：连续控制基准测试（continuous control benchmarks）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于前馈神经网络（feedforward networks），引入观测/特征归一化（observation and feature normalization）、权重归一化（weight normalization）、分布回报建模（distributional returns modeling），与熵正则化MORL算法结合。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：标准连续控制基准测试（standard continuous control benchmarks）；评价指标：解集质量（quality of the produced solution sets），具体指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 实验表明，所提出的架构改进在不改变底层算法的情况下显著提升了MORL解集的质量。摘要未提供具体数值对比或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。摘要未说明在遥感场景下的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人研究方向基本无关，无直接可借鉴点。但其中关于网络架构设计（如归一化技术）对算法性能提升的思路，若未来涉及强化学习驱动的遥感任务（如卫星自主决策、动态目标跟踪等），可参考其架构改进方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep reinforcement learning (RL) have shown that improving neural network architectures can yield substantial gains in sample efficiency and asymptotic performance without altering the underlying algorithms. In contrast, work on multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL), which aims to discover a set of policies that balance trade-offs among conflicting objectives, has predominantly focused on algorithmic innovations, leaving the area of architectures underexplored. While the optimal policies and value functions can differ significantly depending on the trade-offs, MORL algorithms commonly represent them with simple feedforward networks conditioned on the trade-off. This raises the question of whether the performance of the algorithms could be improved with more expressive function approximators. In this paper, we integrate recent advances in neural network design: (i) observation and feature normalization, (ii) weight normalization, and (iii) modeling of distributional returns with an entropy-regularized MORL algorithm. The empirical results across standard continuous control benchmarks demonstrate that these changes substantially improve the quality of the produced solution sets without requiring major changes to the underlying algorithm.

</details>


### [141] [Conformal Fusion Under Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07183)
*Alireza Moayedikia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Modality-Conditioned Conformal Fusion (MCCF)，一种在任意模态缺失下仍能保持校准不确定性的多模态融合架构，通过模态丢弃训练、证据分解和Mondrian共形校准实现形式化的覆盖保证。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦多模态融合中的模态缺失鲁棒性与不确定性校准问题，属于通用机器学习/多模态学习范畴。摘要中未提及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关术语，也未涉及目标检测、轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、边缘部署等与在轨遥感目标检测轻量化直接相关的技术。虽然多模态融合在遥感领域有应用潜力，但论文本身并非面向遥感场景设计，且完全不涉及轻量化或部署问题，因此与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不涉及遥感场景、目标检测任务或轻量化部署技术，阅读优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态融合中的模态缺失鲁棒性与不确定性校准。应用场景：通用多模态分类/预测任务。传感器类型：未限定具体传感器（使用合成数据和三个真实多模态基准）。目标类别：未限定。

Model architecture: MCCF架构包含三个核心组件：(1) 多模态瓶颈融合骨干网络，训练时使用模态丢弃 (modality dropout)；(2) 每模态证据头 (per-modality evidential heads)，产生模态分解的Dirichlet分布；(3) Dempster-Shafer组合规则，将各模态证据融合为联合预测分布。缺失模态贡献空证据 (vacuous evidence)，被结构性地忽略。此外，Mondrian共形校准模块基于模态存在掩码 (modality-presence mask) 提供有限样本组条件覆盖保证。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署可行性分析。

Datasets and metrics: 使用一个合成问题和三个真实多模态基准数据集。评价指标：目标覆盖率 (target coverage)、覆盖差距 (coverage gap)、准确率 (accuracy)。具体数据集名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: MCCF在每种模态存在子集上均能保持目标覆盖率，相比边际分割共形基线 (marginal split-conformal baseline) 显著缩小了完整模态与部分模态之间的覆盖差距，且相对于温度缩放基线 (temperature-scaled baseline) 和证据基线 (evidential baseline) 未引入可测量的准确率代价。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法评估其在遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方面的局限性。摘要未说明计算开销、推理速度等关键部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 尽管论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化基本无关，但以下思路可能有间接借鉴价值：(1) 模态丢弃训练 (modality dropout) 的思想可类比于遥感多模态数据（如光学+SAR）中某传感器失效时的鲁棒性设计；(2) 证据分解与Dempster-Shafer融合规则可用于遥感多源融合中的不确定性量化；(3) Mondrian共形校准提供组条件覆盖保证的思路可用于星上模型在数据分布偏移下的可靠性评估。但以上均为间接启发，论文本身不提供轻量化或目标检测方面的直接可借鉴点。

Abstract: Multimodal fusion architectures typically assume all modalities are available at inference, yet sensor failures, acquisition variability, and cost constraints routinely produce incomplete observations. Existing work treats modality absence as a prediction-accuracy problem, leaving a more basic question unanswered: whether a model's confidence estimates remain calibrated when an entire input stream is removed. We argue that missing-modality robustness and calibrated uncertainty are a single coupled property, and introduce Modality-Conditioned Conformal Fusion (MCCF), an architecture that addresses both at once. MCCF combines a multimodal bottleneck fusion backbone trained with modality dropout, per-modality evidential heads producing modality-decomposed Dirichlet distributions, and a Dempster-Shafer combination rule that fuses the per-modality evidence into a joint predictive distribution; an absent modality contributes vacuous evidence that is structurally ignored, so the fused uncertainty automatically reflects the reduced information without test-time imputation. A Mondrian conformal calibration module keyed on the modality-presence mask then provides finite-sample group-conditional coverage for every non-empty modality subset. MCCF is, to our knowledge, the first method with formal coverage guarantees under arbitrary modality availability through architectural integration rather than post-hoc recalibration, and the evidential decomposition yields per-modality vacuity scores that localise uncertainty to the absent modality responsible. Across a synthetic problem and three real multimodal benchmarks, MCCF holds its target coverage on every modality-presence subset, substantially narrows the coverage gap between full and partial modalities relative to a marginal split-conformal baseline, and imposes no measurable accuracy cost relative to temperature-scaled and evidential baselines.

</details>


### [142] [MAUPITI: On-Device Prototype-Based Learning on a Smart Infrared Sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07192)
*Beatrice Alessandra Motetti,Tanguy Dugas du Villard,Matteo Risso,Alessio Burrello,Francesco Daghero,Enrico Macii,Massimo Poncino,Marco Castellano,Alfio Basile,Daniele Jahier Pagliari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于16×16 TMOS低分辨率红外阵列传感器和RISC-V微控制器的嵌入式人体感知系统，采用离线量化CNN编码器+在线原型更新的NCM分类器，在<32kB内存和~1.5mW功耗下实现姿态/手势识别与持续自适应。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文面向的是嵌入式人体感知（privacy-preserving human sensing），使用低分辨率红外阵列传感器，任务为姿态/手势识别，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、SAR/高光谱/多光谱、目标检测、星上部署等核心方向无任何关联。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：人体姿态识别（pose recognition）和手势识别（gesture recognition）；场景：嵌入式隐私保护人体感知；传感器：16×16低分辨率红外TMOS阵列；目标类别：人体姿态/手势类别。

Model architecture: CNN编码器（用于特征提取）+ NCM（Nearest Class Mean）原型分类器。CNN离线训练和量化，NCM原型在设备上以流式方式存储和更新。

Lightweight method: 涉及模型量化（quantization，CNN离线量化）、低精度SIMD指令（RISC-V扩展）、原型更新替代反向传播和重放缓冲区以节省内存。但这是针对嵌入式人体感知的轻量化，非遥感目标检测轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 部署平台：RISC-V微控制器（扩展低精度SIMD指令）；内存约束：<32kB片上内存；功耗：~1.5mW；延迟开销：分类和原型更新阶段均<0.29%。但这是嵌入式MCU部署，非星上/边缘部署遥感模型。

Datasets and metrics: 两个数据集（摘要未说明具体名称和规模）；评价指标：准确率（accuracy），与常规分类器对比。

Experiments: 在两个数据集上，所提方法准确率与常规分类器相当，分类和原型更新的延迟开销均<0.29%。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 仅针对16×16超低分辨率红外传感器，不适用于遥感高分辨率光学/SAR图像；3. 任务为分类（pose/gesture recognition），非目标检测（object detection），更非旋转框/小目标检测；4. 未涉及剪枝、知识蒸馏、NAS等主流轻量化方法。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴点非常有限。唯一可能参考的是：在极端资源约束下（<32kB内存），采用离线量化+在线原型更新的思路，对于星上超轻量模型更新策略有一定启发，但场景差异巨大，直接借鉴价值低。

Abstract: Low-resolution infrared (IR) array sensors represent an interesting solution for privacy-preserving human sensing in embedded systems. In this letter, we describe a smart multi-pixel IR sensor integrating a 16$\times$16 thermal MOSFET (TMOS) array and a RISC-V microcontroller extended with low-precision SIMD instructions, capable of on-device learning and continual adaptation for pose and gesture recognition tasks under tight memory and power constraints ($<$32kB on-chip memory, $\approx$1.5mW). To avoid the memory overheads of backpropagation and replay buffers, we adopt a prototype-based Nearest Class Mean (NCM) classifier in which a simple Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) encoder is trained and quantized offline, while class prototypes are stored and updated on the device in streaming mode. With experiments on two datasets, we show that this approach yields accuracy on par with a conventional classifier, with negligible latency overheads in both the classification and the prototype update ($<$0.29% considering both phases), effectively enabling online adaptation of the perception framework.

</details>


### [143] [An AI4AI Framework for Visual Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07193)
*Zhen Liu,Wenli Huang,Wei Song,Yuhan Liu,Zhiqin Yang,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AutoPrune，一个基于LLM驱动的视觉token剪枝策略自动设计框架（AI4AI），通过Token Pruning Domain-Specific Language (TPDSL)将搜索状态表示为强基线的残差修改，在14个多模态基准和3个MLLM骨干上实现去除94.4%视觉token仍保留99%以上性能、FLOPs降低9.9x、prefill延迟降低6.4x。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于泛视觉/泛机器学习方法，核心是multimodal large language models (MLLMs)的visual-token pruning，属于LLM驱动的算法自动设计（AI4AI），并非遥感/卫星场景。虽然"pruning"和"FLOPs"与轻量化方向有概念交集，但剪枝对象是视觉token而非模型参数/通道，且不涉及遥感目标检测。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题相关性弱。

Reading priority: 2分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向相关性弱，属于MLLM视觉token剪枝的AI4AI方法。但其"LLM自动设计策略+DSL+残差搜索状态"的方法论对轻量化策略自动搜索有一定借鉴价值，可作为方法论参考，非核心必读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：MLLM的visual-token pruning（视觉token剪枝）策略自动设计。场景：多模态大语言模型推理加速，非遥感场景。传感器类型：摘要未说明（涉及视觉token，非遥感传感器）。目标类别：摘要未说明（非目标检测任务）。

Model architecture: AutoPrune框架：核心是Token Pruning Domain-Specific Language (TPDSL)，包含131个可复用原子（atoms），用于budget control、token scoring、selection constraints、token reassembly。关键设计：将每个搜索状态表示为强base policy的residual modification（残差修改），以缩小搜索空间并引导LLM关注对性能最关键的策略组件。基于三个MLLM backbone进行验证。

Lightweight method: 采用visual-token pruning（视觉token剪枝）作为推理加速手段，属于token级剪枝而非参数/通道级剪枝。方法为training-free（免训练）的LLM-driven策略自动设计（AI4AI），通过TPDSL语言自动生成剪枝策略，替代固定手工启发式规则。不涉及传统pruning/quantization/knowledge distillation/NAS等模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提供了部署相关指标：去除94.4%视觉token时，FLOPs降低9.9x，prefill latency降低6.4x，且保留超过99%的full-token性能。但未说明参数量、显存、能耗、具体延迟数值、是否适配边缘/星上部署。由于面向MLLM而非遥感检测模型，且未涉及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等硬件部署，星上部署可行性摘要未说明。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：14个multimodal benchmarks，3个MLLM backbones。评价指标：性能保持率（保留99%以上full-token性能）、FLOPs降低倍数（9.9x）、prefill latency降低倍数（6.4x）。具体数据集名称和指标细节摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在14个多模态基准和3个MLLM骨干上验证了AutoPrune的有效性、效率和可迁移性（transferability）。核心结果：去除94.4%视觉token时保留>99%性能，FLOPs降低9.9x，prefill latency降低6.4x。消融实验和对比方法细节摘要未说明。

Limitations: 摘要未说明：具体数据集名称、backbone名称、与现有剪枝方法的定量对比、计算开销（LLM生成策略本身的成本）、是否适用于遥感/目标检测场景、参数量/显存/能耗等部署细节。核心局限在于其面向MLLM的token剪枝，与遥感在轨目标检测的轻量化（参数/通道剪枝、量化、蒸馏）在对象和场景上差异较大。

Ideas for my research: 1. AI4AI思路可借鉴：用LLM自动设计轻量化策略（如剪枝/量化策略）替代手工调参，可迁移到遥感检测模型的轻量化策略搜索。2. "residual modification of base policy"的搜索状态表示思想，可用于将轻量化策略表示为对强基线（如YOLO/RT-DETR等）的残差修改，缩小搜索空间。3. Domain-Specific Language (DSL)设计思想可用于定义遥感检测轻量化操作的原子集合（如通道剪枝、量化位宽、蒸馏温度等）。4. token-level pruning思想可迁移到遥感小目标检测中冗余背景token的剔除，但需注意遥感小目标信息密度低，激进token剪枝可能丢失关键小目标，需谨慎。

Abstract: Visual-token pruning can substantially reduce the inference cost of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), yet existing methods largely rely on fixed, handcrafted heuristics and costly expert trial and error. As pruning objectives, budgets, and model architectures diversify, manually navigating the expanding design space becomes increasingly difficult. This paper aims to build an AI4AI framework for visual-token pruning by addressing a natural question: Can large language models automatically design effective visual-token reduction algorithms? Although LLMs possess broad algorithmic knowledge and strong reasoning capabilities, translating such general knowledge into effective solutions for a specialized task remains nontrivial. We argue that the key lies in designing an appropriate search-state representation that connects the internal knowledge of LLMs with the structural requirements and constraints of visual-token pruning. Based on this insight, we propose AutoPrune, a training-free framework for LLM-driven visual-token pruning policy design. At its core, AutoPrune introduces a Token Pruning Domain-Specific Language (TPDSL) comprising 131 reusable atoms for budget control, token scoring, selection constraints, and token reassembly. A key property of TPDSL is that it represents each search state as a residual modification of a strong base policy. This residual formulation narrows the search space and directs the LLM's attention toward the policy components that are most consequential for performance. Experiments on 14 multimodal benchmarks and three MLLM backbones demonstrate the effectiveness, efficiency, and transferability of AutoPrune. Even when removing 94.4% of visual tokens, AutoPrune preserves more than 99% of full-token performance while reducing FLOPs by 9.9x and prefill latency by 6.4x.

</details>


### [144] [Stochastic Autoregressive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07224)
*Ilan Doron-Arad,Idan Mehalel,Elchanan Mossel*

Abstract: Motivated by LLMs, which generate outputs by iteratively sampling from next-token distributions, we introduce a PAC-learning model for binary stochastic autoregressive learning. This generalizes the deterministic autoregressive learning framework of Joshi et al., COLT 2025. In our model, one fixed generator assigns a Bernoulli next-token distribution to every prompt string. Starting from an input prompt, a token is sampled and appended to the prompt; the same generator is then applied again to this expanded prompt; this procedure is repeated for $M$ steps. Three forms of supervision are considered: base one-step samples, chain-of-thought (CoT) samples that reveal full random trajectories of length $M$, and end-to-end (e2e) samples that reveal only the final token of length $M$ trajectories. For a generator class, we study the minimum number of samples $m_{base}(\varepsilon),m_{CoT}(\varepsilon), m_{e2e}(\varepsilon)$, resp., required to learn the one-step probabilities in the base model, and the final-token probability in the CoT and e2e models, under squared loss error~$\varepsilon$.
  We show that stochastic autoregressive learning fundamentally differs from the deterministic theory. At scale $\varepsilon$, there is no universal comparison between the three learning tasks: both $m_{CoT}/m_{base}$ and $m_{e2e}/m_{CoT}$ can be made simultaneously arbitrarily larger than $M/\varepsilon$, the natural analogue for the existing deterministic results. Nevertheless, after altering scales, for every class, CoT learning at scale $\varepsilon$ is upper-bounded by base learning at scale $\varepsilon/M^2$, whereas e2e learning at scale $\varepsilon$ is upper-bounded, up to logarithmic factors, by $(M/\varepsilon) m_{CoT}(Θ(\varepsilon))$. These dependencies and scales are essentially tight. We complement these bounds by studying dimension $d$ logistic functions in our model.

</details>

Abstract: When a reinforcement learning agent cannot observe the full state, we usually blame its policies: it cannot see enough to represent a good one. We show that in a solvable case the bigger problem lies elsewhere. Even when a good policy is available and the agent's value function is expressive enough to describe it exactly, learning still ends up somewhere far worse.
  We study a partially observed linear-quadratic problem in which a standard actor-critic learner can be solved in closed form. At our default setting the best policy the agent can represent is already close to optimal, costing 10.4% more than the ideal controller that observes everything. Learning does not find it. The algorithm instead comes to rest at a policy that is 35% worse than the best one available to it, and we can say exactly where and why.
  The cause is a bias in what the critic learns rather than a limit on what the actor can express. Because the agent cannot attribute what it sees to the part of the state it cannot observe, the critic misreads that unexplained variation as sharp curvature in its own value estimates, and the actor follows that error away from the optimum. We derive closed-form expressions for the resulting policy, for its cost, and for the one design choice that removes the problem, which is how far the learner looks ahead before trusting its own value estimates. Deep reinforcement learning experiments follow these predictions closely. Notably, giving the agent memory of past observations does not help, while changing how far it looks ahead does.

</details>


### [146] [TOFD: Target-Oriented Feature Decoupling against Poisoning Attacks in Split Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07274)

Abstract: Split Federated Learning (SFL) facilitates privacy-preserving collaborative training with reduced client-side overhead. However, its split architecture introduces unique attack surfaces, rendering it vulnerable to diverse poisoning attacks. Most existing defenses fail to exploit the split paradigm, limiting their ability to detect and contain malicious behaviors at an early stage. To bridge this gap, we propose Target-Oriented Feature Decoupling (TOFD), a unified framework that jointly enables proactive detection and robust optimization against a wide range of poisoning attacks. TOFD operates in three stages: (1) Target Inference, which identifies potential attack targets by refining class-wise safe zones via class-specific Margin Perturbation (MP); (2) Sample Purification, which adaptively filters poisoned smashed data using thresholds calibrated through cross-class min-max normalization of MP; and (3) Decoupling Optimization, which leverages an adversarial guidance model to capture attack-induced patterns and decouple their influence during optimization, thereby suppressing residual adversarial effects. We provide theoretical guarantees for the convergence of TOFD. Extensive experiments on five datasets demonstrate that TOFD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art defenses under diverse attack scenarios, achieving superior robustness with low computational overhead suitable for practical deployment.

</details>

Experiments: NeuroSTORM 在头痛判别上优于 FC 矩阵方法（AUROC 0.82 vs 0.67）。多分类中能区分慢性偏头痛，但难以区分其他头痛亚型与慢性偏头痛。在有限数据条件下，NeuroSTORM 能捕获可迁移的潜在表示，无需依赖 FC 特征。

Abstract: Headache is the most common neurological disorder in children and substantially affects quality of life. We investigated whether resting-state functional MRI (rs-fMRI) can support pediatric headache classification using machine learning. We encoded rs-fMRI data using NeuroSTORM, a recent foundation model, and fine-tuned it to distinguish healthy controls from children with headache and subsequently classify headache subtypes. We compared NeuroSTORM with a standard neuroscience approach using functional-connectivity (FC) matrices derived from brain activity as predictors. Using 189 rs-fMRI scans from 110 individuals collected across two visits (prevalence of any headache: 74%), NeuroSTORM achieved an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.82 (95% CI, 0.82-0.82) and an area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) of 0.93 (95% CI, 0.93-0.94) for discriminating headache from non-headache. In contrast, models trained on FC matrices showed lower performance (AUROC, 0.67 [95% CI, 0.67-0.67]; AUPRC, 0.85 [95% CI, 0.85-0.85]). In multiclass classification of healthy controls, chronic migraine, and non-chronic headaches (e.g., post-viral headache, new daily persistent headache, post-traumatic headache), NeuroSTORM achieved a macro-AUROC of 0.69 (95% CI, 0.68-0.69). Results suggest that the approach can distinguish chronic migraine but has difficulty differentiating other headache subtypes from chronic migraine. Overall, under limited-data conditions, NeuroSTORM appears to capture latent rs-fMRI representations that transfer to headache-related tasks without relying on FC features. These findings provide proof of concept for fMRI-based prediction of pediatric headache and highlight potential future utility for subtype identification and individualized treatment strategies.

</details>


### [148] [FUSE: Feature-Wise Unified Specialization with Cross-Column Exchange for Mixed-Type Tabular Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07294)
*Suman Cha,Seongchan Lee,Dohyun Ko,Hyunjoong Kim*

Abstract: Generating mixed-type tabular data requires jointly modeling diverse feature distributions and their complex cross-column dependencies. Variational flow matching handles distinct endpoints via factorized distributions, yet leaves feature-specific processing and cross-column interactions implicit within a shared backbone. We introduce Feature-wise Unified Specialization with cross-column Exchange (FUSE) to explicitly separate these roles. FUSE applies separate adaptive mixture modules to numerical and categorical features, allowing each feature to combine shared specialized subnetworks, while joint attention preserves information exchange across all columns. We also characterize the excess population risk from restricted conditioning contexts and bound the continuous Wasserstein generation error by endpoint-prediction risk. Comprehensive experiments on eight tabular datasets demonstrate that FUSE achieves strong and consistent performance across distributional fidelity and downstream utility metrics.

</details>


### [149] [From Optimal Actions to World Models: Identifiability of Transition Kernels in Discounted MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07301)

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: We study what can be recovered about the transition probabilities of a Markov decision process from optimal actions alone. This is closely related to the inverse problem considered by Letcher et al., who ask when the dynamics can be recovered from numerical \(Q\)-values. Here the numerical values themselves are not observed; only the optimal actions are known, for every reward in a given class.
  For state-action rewards \(r(s,a)\), knowing the optimal actions for every reward also tells us how much better one action is than another when each is followed by the same fixed policy. This is still not enough to determine the transition probabilities uniquely. We prove that two kernels give the same optimal actions for every reward exactly when \[ Q_{s,a} =
\Bigl(P_{s,a}+\tfrac1γe_s^{\mathsf T}(L-I)\Bigr)L^{-1} \] for one invertible matrix \(L\) satisfying \(L\mathbf 1=\mathbf 1\). Near a kernel with strictly positive entries, there is an \(n(n-1)\)-dimensional family of different kernels with this property. The result is unchanged if we consider only rewards having a unique optimal action at every state.
  We then compare this with rewards of the forms \(r(s)\) and \(r(s,a,s')\). Rewards that depend on the next state can usually recover the transition kernel itself: every row at a state with at least two actions is determined, and we describe exactly when a row at a state with one action can remain hidden. State rewards reveal less: two kernels give the same optimal actions exactly when every deterministic policy is optimal for the same set of rewards. The results show how the form of the reward affects what can be learned about the dynamics from optimal actions alone.

</details>

Experiments: 摘要仅说明在多种架构和数据集上验证了方法，证明其能保持模型零样本性能（zero-shot performance）并确保模型更新间的兼容性。具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但明显局限在于：论文面向检索/表示兼容性场景，与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向无直接关联；未涉及任何部署相关指标。

Abstract: Retrieval systems rely on representations learned by increasingly powerful models. However, due to the high training cost and inconsistencies in learned representations, there is significant interest in facilitating communication between representations and ensuring compatibility across independently trained neural networks. In the literature, two primary approaches are commonly used to adapt different learned representations: affine transformations, which adapt well to specific distributions but can significantly alter the original representation, and orthogonal transformations, which preserve the original structure with strict geometric constraints but limit adaptability. A key challenge is adapting the latent spaces of updated models to align with those of previous models on downstream distributions while preserving the newly learned representation spaces. In this paper, we impose a relaxed orthogonality constraint, namely $λ$-Orthogonality regularization, while learning an affine transformation, to obtain distribution-specific adaptation while retaining the original learned representations. Extensive experiments across various architectures and datasets validate our approach, demonstrating that it preserves the model's zero-shot performance and ensures compatibility across model updates. Code available at: \href{https://github.com/miccunifi/lambda_orthogonality.git}{https://github.com/miccunifi/lambda\_orthogonality}.

</details>


### [151] [Is SwiGLU's Open Positive Tail Necessary? Evidence from Closed-Tail Gating with MemGLU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07323)
*Yuting Ge,Pengju Yang,Mingkai Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 decoder-only 语言模型 FFN 中 SwiGLU 激活函数的开放正尾部是否必要，提出 MemGLU 作为闭尾对照，实验表明在 9M/30M 规模下 MemGLU 与 SwiGLU 验证 NLL 相差约 0.1%，说明开放正尾部并非必需。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2 分）。论文研究对象是 decoder-only 语言模型（LLM）的 FFN 激活函数（SwiGLU vs MemGLU），属于自然语言处理/大语言模型领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关系。既非遥感场景，也非目标检测任务，更不涉及剪枝/量化/蒸馏等轻量化方法。唯一可能的间接借鉴点是激活函数设计对模型容量与效率的影响，但相关性很弱。

Reading priority: 1 分。论文聚焦 decoder-only 语言模型 FFN 激活函数机制，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，无直接可借鉴的检测方法、轻量化技术或部署指标，仅作背景了解即可。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：语言模型（decoder-only LLM）的 FFN 激活函数机制研究。场景：NLP/大语言模型预训练，非遥感场景。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: decoder-only 语言模型，FFN 采用 SwiGLU 激活函数；提出 MemGLU 作为闭尾（closed-tail）对照激活函数，源自 memristive branch geometry。模型规模为 9M 和 30M 参数。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。本文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS 等轻量化方法。仅涉及激活函数几何形态（开放正尾部 vs 闭尾）对模型行为的影响，与模型压缩/加速无直接关系。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量（除 9M/30M 预训练规模外）、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署信息。且该工作面向语言模型，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标为 validation NLL（验证负对数似然）。实验设置：9M 和 30M 预训练，各三个随机种子（three seeds）配对运行。

Experiments: 主要结果：MemGLU 与 SwiGLU 在验证 NLL 上相差约 0.1%。训练好的 SwiGLU checkpoint 对正尾部抑制敏感；机制诊断显示两种模型虽损失相近但 gate 使用方式不同。结论：在测试规模下，SwiGLU 的开放正尾部对 decoder-only 语言模型 FFN 并非必要。

Limitations: 摘要未说明。但可推断：实验规模较小（9M/30M），未覆盖更大规模模型；仅针对语言模型 FFN，未涉及视觉/遥感任务；未提供部署相关指标；MemGLU 的 memristive 硬件实现可行性未在摘要中说明。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：1) 激活函数设计会影响模型对特定几何结构的适应能力，若在遥感目标检测轻量化网络中探索更高效的激活函数（如替代 GELU/SiLU），可能带来参数量或计算量的微小收益；2) 论文的"机制诊断"思路（分析 gate 使用方式）可迁移到轻量化检测模型中分析不同模块对压缩的敏感性。但整体相关性很弱，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: We test whether decoder-only language-model FFNs require SwiGLU's open positive tail. We introduce MemGLU as a closed-tail comparator derived from a memristive branch geometry. Across paired 9M and 30M pretraining runs with three seeds, MemGLU remains within about 0.1% of SwiGLU in validation NLL. Trained SwiGLU checkpoints are sensitive to positive-tail suppression, while mechanism diagnostics show that the two models use their gates differently despite similar losses. These results suggest that models adapt to the gate geometry available during pretraining. At the tested scales, SwiGLU's open positive tail is not necessary for decoder-only language-model FFNs.

</details>


### [152] [When GNNs Fail: Quantifying and Overcoming Temporal Correlation Volatility in Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07333)
*Chen Shao,Yue Wang,Zhenyi Zhu,Zhanbo Huang,Tobias Käfer,Zonghan Wu,Danai Koutra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Modeling multivariate time series by representing them as graphs, where individual series act as nodes and pairwise temporal corre- lations serve as edges, has gained significant traction. Recent advances in Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have demonstrated strong perfor- mance by assuming a static graph topology and aggregating information from neighboring series. In this work, we investigate the representa- tional power of GNNs for forecasting under both static and dynamic settings (i.e., when pairwise correlations evolve drastically over time) and identify critical limitations in current architectures. To formalize this, we first propose Temporal Correlation Volatility (TCV), a model- agnostic metric designed to quantify the distributional evolution of these latent structures. We establish a clear connection between TCV and performance degradation, demonstrating that many popular models, including Transformers, generalize poorly in high-TCV settings and are often outperformed by simple structure-agnostic baselines. To address these limitations, we propose Graph Layer for Inference in Dynamic En- vironments (GLIDE), a novel GNN layer enhanced by two theoretically grounded design mechanisms: (D1) Path-based Message Passing, which captures path-based neighborhoods and (D2) Static and Dynamic Propagation Separation, which identifies optimal dynamics via local static approximation. These components significantly improve learning under dynamic topology while preserving robustness in static scenarios. Ex- tensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that GLIDE improves average performance by up to 45.6% across static and dynamic settings, with the largest gain reaching 85.7%. The source code is available at https://github.com/ChenS676/GLIDE.

</details>


### [153] [Aftab: A Comprehensive Benchmark of CNN Encoders and Advanced Value Functions in Parallelized Q-Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07335)
*Taha Shieenavaz,Shabnam Zareshahraki,Loris Nanni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Research relevance: Relevance analysis unavailable

Reading priority: Priority unavailable

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: Task and scene extraction unavailable

Model architecture: Model architecture extraction unavailable

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Recent advancements in deep reinforcement learning have increasingly favored simplified, highly parallelized paradigms. Notably, the Parallelized Q-Network (PQN) algorithm achieves stable off-policy learning without relying on computationally expensive replay buffers or target networks. However, the representational capacity and parameter efficiency of visual encoders operating in these buffer-free settings remain underexplored. In this work, we systematically investigate the architectural design space of Convolutional Neural Networks for PQN. We design and rigorously evaluate eight distinct CNN topologies, optimizing for sample efficiency under strict parameter constraints. Furthermore, we study the impact of representation and value estimation enhancements by integrating the Hadamax encoding paradigm and advanced Q-learning extensions, including distributional, ensemble, and dueling heads. Extensive experiments on the Atari-57 benchmark demonstrate that our proposed composite architecture, Aftab, achieves an Interquartile Mean (IQM) Human-Normalized Score of 6.479, establishing a 0.86 Probability of Improvement over the standard PQN baseline. Additionally, structural resilience evaluations on the highly non-stationary Procgen Hard benchmark confirm out-of-distribution generalization, with Aftab yielding an IQM Procgen Normalized Score of 0.418 compared to the baseline's 0.382. Ultimately, this work establishes an efficient, probabilistically superior structural reference for model-free reinforcement learning, all while preserving the simplicity and memory efficiency of unbuffered, parallelized optimization.
  The complete Aftab framework, including all model definitions, training configurations, and raw experimental logs, is open-sourced and available on our GitHub repository: https://github.com/tahashieenavaz/aftab

</details>


### [154] [Residual Algebra for Representation-Preserving Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07349)
*Yao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于代数化残差表示的学习框架，将异构表示建模为带类型（坐标+残差）的对象，通过松弛-聚合-闭合的代数操作进行有序组合，在金融股票数据上提升收益和夏普比率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文处理的是金融时序数据（股票日频观测），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、边缘部署等模型压缩方法。

Reading priority: 1分，不推荐阅读。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，属于金融量化投资领域的表示学习方法，对本课题无参考价值。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：金融时序预测/多源异构表示学习；应用场景：中国A股市场量化投资；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 提出一种代数化残差表示框架：将异构表示建模为带类型（坐标+残差）的对象，通过Fold（10×10秩网格上的条件均值场）实现对象，通过FPRC-PQ（松弛-聚合-闭合）实现代数操作，包含反射性反刍算子（reflective rumination operator）用于全局重构位移的读取与增益修正。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该工作面向金融计算，不涉及星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：3.67M中国A股股票日频观测（2023-2026），采用冻结时点协议（frozen point-in-time protocol）；评价指标：净成本后收益率（net-of-cost return，从13.52%提升至19.10%）、夏普比率（Sharpe ratio，从1.42提升至2.09）。

Experiments: 与匹配容量（matched-capacity）、统一残差（unified-residual）、无身份两阶段（identity-free two-stage）、仅成对（pairwise-only）等对照方法比较，所提方法均优于对照。消融实验表明增益来自显式残差所有权和组合，而非更多特征或更多树。

Limitations: 1. 完全面向金融时序数据，与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化无任何关联；2. 未讨论模型效率、参数量、推理速度等部署相关指标；3. 方法高度定制化于金融多源异构表示场景，迁移到遥感视觉任务的可能性极低。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，无可借鉴点。其核心思想（代数化残差表示、显式类型化组合）属于表示学习理论方向，不适用于遥感目标检测的轻量化需求。

Abstract: Learning from heterogeneous representations is usually reduced to feature concatenation, which erases which representation produced an error. We instead algebraize the residual: a representation is a typed object that owns both a coordinate system and the residual it leaves unresolved, and learning is an ordered composition of operators that preserve or deliberately erase that type. Fold realizes the objects as point-in-time conditional-mean fields on 10x10 rank grids. FPRC-PQ realizes the algebra as relax-aggregate-close: each field is relaxed by a correction fitted to its own residual in its own coordinates; corrected fields meet at a fixed mean that is the sole identity-erasure boundary; and a shared learner closes only the aggregate's fresh residual. The composition telescopes exactly into representation, local residual estimate, and residual-of-residual estimate. Its aggregate is a learned control-variate interface with population variance reduction, while refitting the closer along perturbations of the backbone yields first-order coupled-path mean orthogonality. As an analytical extension, a reflective rumination operator reads the displacement of a global reconstruction from the aggregate anchor, reflects it, and fixes its gain by a unique orthogonal projection rather than return-tuned grid search. On 3.67M Chinese A-share stock-day observations (2023-2026) under a frozen point-in-time protocol, the evaluated base algebra raises net-of-cost return from 13.52% to 19.10% and Sharpe from 1.42 to 2.09. Matched-capacity, unified-residual, identity-free two-stage, and pairwise-only controls all trail it. The gain is therefore not explained by more features or more trees, but by making residual ownership and composition explicit while representation identity is still available.

</details>


### [155] [Trajectory-Relative Hindsight Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07371)
*Haoyu Zheng,Yun Zhu,Qing Wang,Wenqiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRIAL，一种基于轨迹相对事后蒸馏的强化学习框架，通过统一的回合对齐评分协议，将事后奖励信号在决策回合间合理分配，提升WebShop和ALFWorld等环境下的agentic RL性能。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。论文属于agentic reinforcement learning / LLM agent领域，聚焦事后奖励分配与hindsight distillation，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化/部署。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，仅在"蒸馏"这一概念上存在极弱的泛化关联，但并非知识蒸馏用于模型压缩。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，属于LLM agentic RL领域，不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型压缩，仅作泛读了解即可，无需精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：agentic reinforcement learning（智能体强化学习），具体为LLM agent在WebShop和ALFWorld环境中的决策任务。场景：通用web购物与家用任务环境，非遥感场景。传感器类型：不适用（文本/决策环境）。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体网络结构，仅提及使用不同backbone（如Qwen3-1.7B）的LLM agent。核心是TRIAL框架的turn-aligned scoring protocol，通过signed log-probability gap确定token级监督方向与强度，turn级幅度在轨迹上联合归一化。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文中的"distillation"指hindsight distillation（事后蒸馏，一种RL奖励分配机制），并非用于模型压缩的知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）。不涉及剪枝、量化、NAS、轻量网络等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、星上/边缘部署等部署相关信息。仅提及backbone为Qwen3-1.7B（1.7B参数规模），但未讨论部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集/环境：WebShop和ALFWorld。评价指标：success rate（成功率）和task score（任务得分）。实验设置：不同backbone × 环境 × 评价指标共8种组合。

Experiments: TRIAL在全部8种backbone、环境、评价指标组合上优于GRPO；在6种组合上达到6种方法中的最佳或并列最佳。WebShop + Qwen3-1.7B上，成功率从56.4%提升到75.2%，任务得分从78.7%提升到85.7%。消融实验表明轨迹相对回合分配相比单纯dense hindsight distillation带来显著增益。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但明显局限包括：仅验证于WebShop和ALFWorld两个文本决策环境，未涉及视觉/遥感场景；未讨论计算开销、部署成本；未涉及模型压缩或边缘部署；与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值有限。唯一可迁移的抽象思想是"奖励/监督信号在时间或空间维度上的合理分配与归一化"——若未来在遥感目标检测中引入强化学习或自监督训练，可参考其"轨迹相对分配"思想来平衡不同样本/区域的监督强度。但该论文不提供任何遥感、目标检测或轻量化方法，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Recent agentic reinforcement learning methods use hindsight to complement sparse outcome rewards. However, a completed rollout can yield many such signals, leaving their appropriate allocation across turns unclear. We introduce TRIAL, a trajectory-relative hindsight distillation framework with a unified turn-aligned scoring protocol. For each decision turn, TRIAL extracts an outcome view of that decision's realized consequence and evaluates the same response under ordinary and hindsight-conditioned contexts. The signed log-probability gap determines the direction and local strength of token-level supervision, while turn-level magnitudes are normalized jointly over the realized trajectory. The resulting allocation multipliers have an eligible-token-weighted mean of one, redistributing dense supervision across turns while fixing its average multiplier. Experiments on WebShop and ALFWorld with different backbones show that TRIAL outperforms GRPO across all eight combinations of backbone, environment, and evaluation metric, while achieving the best or tied-best performance among six methods on six of them. On WebShop with Qwen3-1.7B, TRIAL improves the success rate from 56.4% to 75.2% and the task score from 78.7% to 85.7%. Controlled ablations further show that trajectory-relative turn allocation provides substantial gains beyond those of dense hindsight distillation alone.

</details>


### [156] [Beyond Post-Hoc Temperature Scaling: Bilevel Optimization for LLM Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07419)
*Ruochen Jin,Zhanliang Wang,Zongyu Dai,Jiancong Xiao,Bojian Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种通过双层优化在训练阶段最大化预测分布熵来改善大语言模型校准性的方法，避免传统温度缩放对域依赖的问题。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。论文聚焦大语言模型（LLM）的校准问题，属于自然语言处理/泛机器学习领域，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关联。既非遥感场景，也非目标检测任务，更不涉及模型压缩或边缘部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：大语言模型校准（calibration）与偏好对齐（preference alignment）。场景：多选问答与开放式生成问答。传感器类型：不适用（文本数据）。目标类别：不适用。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法，仅涉及训练阶段校准优化，与模型压缩或推理加速无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署信息，且LLM本身与星上/边缘部署场景不匹配。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标涉及校准性（calibration）、过置信度（overconfidence）、域外泛化（out-of-domain generalization），实验覆盖多选与开放式生成问答。

Experiments: 实验表明该方法能产生校准良好的LLM，尤其在域外泛化上具有优势。摘要未给出具体数值结果或对比基线细节。

Limitations: 摘要未说明。未涉及遥感场景、目标检测任务、轻量化部署；其校准方法针对文本生成任务，与视觉目标检测的置信度校准问题差异较大，迁移性存疑。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值有限。唯一可参考的点是"最大化预测熵以抑制过置信"的思想，或可类比到遥感目标检测中检测头置信度校准问题（如缓解小目标/旋转框检测的过置信误检），但需自行设计视觉域适配方案。整体与课题方向关联度低。

Abstract: Preference alignment often makes large language models (LLMs) overconfident and poorly calibrated. Traditional post-hoc temperature scaling is inherently domain-dependent: a temperature fitted on one domain does not generalize across domains. This motivates us to modify model parameters during training to improve calibration. We propose maximizing the entropy of predictive distributions as the calibration objective, which directly targets overconfidence by discouraging overly concentrated predictions. Inspired by temperature scaling, we realize this through a bilevel optimization formulation, where the lower level trains the model under a parametric loss and the upper level selects loss hyperparameters to maximize entropy. To make the framework practical at LLM scale, we adopt an efficient first-order approximation that avoids explicit second-order computation. Across both multiple-choice and open-ended generative question answering, experiments demonstrate that our method yields well-calibrated LLMs with particular advantages in out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [157] [Beyond Myopic World Models: Long-Horizon End-to-End Training for Direct Future Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07420)
*Xinyi Li,Zaishuo Xia,Chenjie Hao,Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Direct Prediction World Model (DPWM)，一种非递归世界模型架构，通过端到端终点预测目标直接优化长时程预测精度，避免递归 rollout 带来的误差累积与梯度传播不稳定问题。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于泛机器学习/强化学习领域的世界模型（world model）研究，聚焦连续控制与像素级基准上的长时程预测，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关联。其核心贡献在于训练目标（endpoint prediction）与架构设计（非递归），而非遥感场景或模型压缩。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于世界模型/强化学习领域。仅当研究涉及时序/视频流目标检测的长时程推理稳定性时，其"训练目标与使用尺度匹配"的思想可作间接参考，否则无需深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：长时程世界模型预测（long-horizon endpoint prediction）。场景：continuous-control 和 pixel-based benchmarks（连续控制与像素级基准）。传感器类型：摘要未说明（非遥感传感器）。目标类别：摘要未说明（非目标检测任务）。

Model architecture: DPWM 为非递归架构，将任意长度的 action sequence 压缩为单个 embedding，并在单次前向传播中预测终点观测（endpoint observation）。避免了预测与梯度传播中的递归 rollout，使长时程端到端训练在 unrolled autoregressive training 不稳定的尺度上变得可行。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS 等模型压缩方法。架构上通过非递归设计减少了递归 rollout 的计算开销，但并非面向轻量化/边缘部署的显式压缩手段。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标。非递归单次前向设计在计算效率上可能优于递归 rollout，但论文未给出星上/边缘部署相关数据。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：continuous-control 和 pixel-based benchmarks（具体名称摘要未说明）。评价指标：long-horizon endpoint prediction accuracy（长时程终点预测精度）。实验设置：对比递归世界模型基线，并验证在更长预测时程下增益更大；同时用相同长时程终点目标重训递归基线，验证训练目标而非骨干结构是主要驱动因素。

Experiments: DPWM 在长时程终点预测上显著优于递归世界模型基线，且预测时程越长增益越大。重训实验表明递归基线在采用相同长时程终点目标后同样受益，支持"训练目标而非骨干选择是长时程预测精度的主要驱动因素"这一核心主张。

Limitations: 摘要未说明：具体数据集名称、参数量/计算量、部署指标、遥感场景适用性。论文聚焦世界模型长时程预测，未涉及目标检测、遥感图像理解或模型压缩，与本研究方向无直接交集。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值有限但存在间接启发：(1) "训练目标与评估尺度应匹配最终使用场景"的思想可迁移——在轨检测模型若用于长时间序列（如视频流/多帧时序检测），可考虑以端到端长时程目标而非局部帧损失来训练；(2) 非递归单次前向设计对降低推理延迟有启发，可类比到在轨实时推理中减少递归/循环结构；(3) 误差累积与梯度传播的分析思路可用于理解轻量化模型在长时程推理中的稳定性。但整体上该论文不提供遥感、目标检测或轻量化压缩的具体方法。

Abstract: World models are expected to support imagination over extended temporal horizons, yet most are still trained through local few-step prediction objectives and deployed by recursively rolling out their own predictions. This creates a fundamental mismatch: few-step losses optimize local transition fidelity, while long-horizon prediction depends on how errors and gradients propagate through the entire trajectory. As a result, transitions with different downstream influence on the endpoint are treated uniformly during training, and small local errors are amplified through recursive inference. We argue that long-horizon accuracy is better achieved by optimizing directly, through an end-to-end endpoint prediction objective. To instantiate this paradigm, we introduce the Direct Prediction World Model (DPWM), a non-recursive architecture that compresses an action sequence of arbitrary length into a single embedding and predicts the endpoint observation in a single forward pass. This design avoids recurrent rollout in both prediction and gradient propagation, making long-horizon end-to-end training practical at horizons where unrolled autoregressive training becomes unstable. Empirically, DPWM substantially improves long-horizon endpoint prediction over recursive world-model baselines on continuous-control and pixel-based benchmarks, with larger gains as the prediction horizon increases. We further show that recurrent baselines benefit similarly when retrained with the same long-horizon endpoint objective, supporting our central claim that the training objective, rather than the particular backbone choice, is the main driver of long-horizon prediction accuracy. Our results suggest that world models can benefit from being trained and evaluated at the temporal scales where they are ultimately used, shifting the focus from local transition modeling toward long-horizon predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [158] [Diffusion LLMs as Targets and Adversaries: Mechanistic Safety Exploits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07430)
*Elena Dumitrescu,Gert Lek,Lydia Y. Chen,Jérémie Decouchant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散大语言模型（DLLMs）的安全对齐机制脆弱性，提出基于安全神经元引导的离线黑盒越狱框架，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文聚焦于大语言模型的安全对齐与越狱攻击，属于AI安全/大模型安全领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。虽然提到了pruning（剪枝），但这里的剪枝是针对安全神经元的攻击手段，而非模型压缩轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型安全对齐分析、越狱攻击；应用场景：AI安全；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 扩散大语言模型（DLLMs），包括LLaDA、Dream、Fast-dLLM等，采用迭代并行去噪替代自回归逐token预测。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到的pruning（剪枝）是用于安全神经元映射和转移攻击的手段，不是模型压缩或加速的轻量化方法。不涉及量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 攻击成功率（ASR）、AUROC（区分良性/越狱提示的曲线下面积）。数据集信息摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在LLaDA、Dream、Fast-dLLM、Llama-3-8B-Instruct、Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct、Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite等模型上实验。自剪枝使ASR从2.6%升至73.8%（LLaDA），从1.9%升至86.6%（Dream）；转移剪枝使ASR从1.9%升至73.2%（Dream），从7.0%升至86.3%（Fast-dLLM）。SN-Guided Diffusion方法在多个模型上达到最高77.1%-86.9%的转移ASR，仅需每提示20次生成。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何直接参考。摘要未说明模型计算开销、部署可行性等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。虽然论文使用了"pruning"一词，但这是安全攻击手段，与模型轻量化中的剪枝（去除冗余权重/通道以减小模型体积和加速推理）完全不同。不建议参考。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (DLLMs) replace autoregressive next-token prediction with iterative parallel denoising, yet their internal safety mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this work, we investigate DLLMs both as targets and as adversaries, exposing mechanistic vulnerabilities in diffusion-based alignment.
  We first show that safety alignment in DLLMs remains sparse and transferable across architectures. DLLMs initialized from autoregressive predecessors inherit the same mechanistic safety footprint as their source models, enabling transfer attacks via direct safety neuron mapping and pruning. Self-pruning increases attack success rates (ASR) from 2.6% to 73.8% on LLaDA and from 1.9% to 86.6% on Dream, while transfer pruning from Qwen2.5 increases ASR from 1.9% to 73.2% on Dream and from 7.0% to 86.3% on Fast-dLLM.
  Building on these findings, we introduce SN-Guided Diffusion, a fully offline black-box jailbreak framework that steers the diffusion process away from safety-triggering regions using a weighted safety neuron loss, which achieves near-perfect prompt separability (AUROC = 1.0 for benign-vs-jailbreak discrimination). Across multiple open and proprietary targets, our method achieves a transfer ASR of up to 77.1% on Llama-3-8B-Instruct, 86.9% on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and 74.3% against Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite, while requiring only 20 generation episodes per prompt. Compared to prior jailbreaking frameworks, our method achieves competitive transferability with orders-of-magnitude lower generation cost.
  Our codebase is available at https://github.com/ellyoana/sn-guided-diffusion.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Fast and Accurate: An Adaptive VLA Inference Framework through Environment-aware Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06434)
*Yuewei Sun,Lang Qin,Zechuan Tian,Jingwen Li,Guiqin Wang,Shengzeng Huo,Wenxin Ren,Tao Fang,Xiaochen Zhang,Guanqing Deng,Xiang Wang,Xiaowen Dong,Qinghai Guo,Yuxin Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种环境感知模型选择（EMS）框架，在双系统VLA架构中通过强化学习策略在大型推理系统与轻量反应系统之间动态切换，平衡任务成功率与推理速度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于具身智能（embodied intelligence）中的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，场景为机器人操作（LIBERO基准、双臂操作），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱或空间在轨智能。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：具身智能中的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）推理；应用场景：机器人操作（单臂/双臂操作）；传感器类型：视觉（RGB）；目标类别：无目标检测任务。摘要未说明遥感相关场景。

Model architecture: 双系统VLA架构：大型推理系统（deliberative system）负责全局轨迹规划，轻量反应系统（reactive system）负责高频闭环控制。两个系统完全解耦，通过强化学习策略进行环境感知的模型选择（EMS）。

Lightweight method: 涉及轻量化的思想：通过环境感知的模型选择（EMS）在大型模型和轻量模型之间动态切换，稀疏使用大型模型以降低整体推理开销。但这不是传统的模型压缩方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS），而是推理时模型选择策略。摘要未说明轻量反应系统的具体轻量化手段（如参数量、FLOPs等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提到有效动作频率达到93.4 Hz（LIBERO基准），但这是机器人控制频率，与星上部署无关。不涉及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等边缘部署技术。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：LIBERO基准（机器人操作）；评价指标：任务成功率（success rate）、有效动作频率（effective action frequency, 93.4 Hz）。摘要未说明遥感相关数据集。

Experiments: 在LIBERO基准上，EMS达到与大型基线相当的成功率，同时将有效动作频率提升至93.4 Hz。在真实世界双臂操作任务中，加速任务完成并保持稳健性能。摘要未说明与遥感目标检测相关的实验。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/航空图像场景；2. 不涉及目标检测任务；3. 轻量化方法仅为推理时模型选择策略，非模型压缩；4. 未提供参数量、FLOPs等量化指标；5. 场景为机器人操作，与在轨遥感目标检测差异巨大。

Ideas for my research: 弱借鉴点：双系统动态切换的思想——在星上资源受限场景下，可考虑设计一个轻量检测模型常驻运行，仅在检测到复杂场景或高置信度需求时调用大型模型，类似EMS的稀疏调用策略。但整体相关性极低，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence demands both long-horizon reasoning and real-time closed-loop responsiveness. Recent dual-system Vision-Language-Action (VLA) architectures combine fast reactive control with slow deliberative reasoning to balance inference speed and task success rate. However, existing dual-process VLAs tightly couple the fast module to intermediate representations of the slow module, necessitating end-to-end joint training and limiting modularity, extensibility and flexible system switching. In this paper, we propose Environment-aware Model Selection (EMS), an adaptive VLA inference framework that switches between two fully decoupled systems of different scales through environment-aware model selection. The large-scale deliberative system provides globally consistent trajectory planning to ensure task success, while a lightweight reactive system enables high-frequency closed-loop control. A reinforcement-learning-based switching policy dynamically selects which system to invoke based on real-time feedback, enabling sparse use of the slow system and thereby balancing pretrained knowledge utilisation with runtime efficiency. Our design offers three key advantages over prior hierarchical VLA frameworks: (1) a fully decoupled and modular dual-system architecture that supports plug-and-play model replacement; (2) an adaptive, environment-aware switching strategy; (3) high-frequency inference for responsive closed-loop control. We extensively evaluate EMS in both simulation and real-world environments. On the LIBERO benchmark, EMS achieves success rates comparable to the large-scale baseline while increasing the effective action frequency to 93.4 Hz. The framework further demonstrates strong extensibility in real-world dual-arm manipulation tasks, where it accelerates task completion while maintaining robust performance.

</details>


### [160] [LyEvO: Lyapunov-Guided Evolutionary Optimization for Safe and Robust Sim-to-Real Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06481)
*Riccardo Curcio,Hongpeng Cao,Marco Caccamo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LyEvO框架，结合Lyapunov稳定性分析与约束进化优化、统计模型检验，用于安全鲁棒的仿真到现实迁移控制策略训练与部署就绪评估。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人控制领域的sim-to-real迁移，涉及Cartpole和3D Quadrotor控制策略的安全性与鲁棒性验证，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：控制策略训练与sim-to-real迁移；应用场景：机器人控制（Cartpole、3D Quadrotor）；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: LyEvO框架：结合Lyapunov稳定性分析、约束进化优化、统计模型检验（SMC）的迭代优化-验证流程。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及通过仿真和真实实验验证了sim-to-real迁移的可行性与安全性。

Datasets and metrics: 基准测试：Cartpole和3D Quadrotor仿真环境；评价指标：安全性与鲁棒性相关的指标（摘要未具体说明指标名称）。

Experiments: 在Cartpole和3D Quadrotor上进行了大量仿真实验和针对性真实世界实验，验证了安全鲁棒的sim-to-real迁移效果。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化技术。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人控制领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无交集。

Abstract: Training controllers that are safe and robust in simulation, and systematically assessing their readiness for real-world deployment, remain key challenges in sim-to-real transfer. To address this, we propose LyEvO, a physics-grounded framework that combines constrained Evolutionary Optimization and Statistical Model Checking (SMC)-based verification with Lyapunov-based stability analysis. Leveraging prior knowledge of the system dynamics, LyEvO uses Lyapunov analysis to compute an initial candidate stability region. An iterative loop then uses operational scenarios drawn from this region to jointly optimize and statistically verify a policy, and subsequently expands the region's boundaries based on the verification outcome. This integrated procedure provides a practical criterion for assessing deployment readiness. We evaluate LyEvO on Cartpole and 3D Quadrotor benchmarks through extensive simulations and targeted real-world experiments, demonstrating safe and robust sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [161] [A Disturbance in the Force: Force Actuation on the RAVEN II Surgical Robot with Parallel Motor-Cable Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06488)
*Haonan Peng,Dun-Tin Chiang,Jordan Hendricks,Andrew Lewis,Jared Shing,Haokun Feng,Yun-Hsuan Su,Blake Hannaford*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一套并联电机-缆绳系统，用于为手术机器人提供可控外部力，以采集学习型力估计所需的代表性训练数据，初步实验力驱动误差小于1N。

Research relevance: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文主题是手术机器人的触觉反馈与力估计，属于医疗机器人/机器人控制领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、地球观测、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为手术机器人力估计，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，无需深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型结构。核心是硬件系统（并联电机-缆绳单元）而非神经网络架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集；评价指标：力驱动误差（小于1N）。

Experiments: 初步实验表明力驱动精度误差小于1N。摘要未说明对比方法或消融实验。

Limitations: 摘要仅报告初步实验，未说明系统在复杂工况下的鲁棒性、力估计模型的精度、训练数据规模等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。唯一可参考的点是"如何为模型训练构建可控、多样化的数据采集系统"这一方法论思路，但与本课题的遥感目标检测和轻量化方向无直接关联。

Abstract: Difficulty in haptic feedback for surgical robots has been a long-term problem for decades. In recent years, learning-based force estimation from robot states suggests desirable accuracy without the necessity of extra sensors. However, challenges remain in obtaining representative training data in which the robot moves in the workspace under various external forces. In this work, a parallel motor-cable system is developed. With six motor-cable units installed around the robot workspace, cables with controllable tension connected to the robot end-effector can provide the desired external force without interfering with the movement of the surgical robot. The development of the system includes motor-unit hardware, control software, sensor drivers, simulations, and more. Preliminary experiments suggest an accuracy of force actuation with errors less than 1 N.

</details>


### [162] [SyncSBC: Decentralized Swarm Behavior Prediction for Synchronized Autonomous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06587)
*Varun Raveendra,Connor Mattson,Daniel S. Brown*

Abstract: Robot swarms utilize many independent limited-sensing agents to produce complex emergent behaviors without requiring centralized control. However, little research explores how agents can infer swarm-level behavior from purely local perception, a capability critical for detecting faults and behavior changes. In this paper, we introduce Synchronized Swarm Behavior Classification (SyncSBC), which combines improvements in machine learning and distributed consensus to classify collective swarm behavior and synchronize swarm decision-making in an entirely decentralized manner. We show that SyncSBC achieves high classification accuracy and low synchronization delay, making it suitable for real-world deployment. Finally, we use SyncSBC to demonstrate two promising swarm applications on real robots where we show that swarms utilizing SyncSBC can accurately identify anomalies in robot behavior and autonomously coordinate collective changes in swarm behavior. Videos, code and supplemental experiments are available at https://sites.google.com/view/sync-sbc/home.

</details>

Abstract: This paper presents a real-time Plan-and-Avoid (PAA framework for coordinating cooperative multi-agent airspace operations around a declared priority trajectory. The priority trajectory represents an aircraft flight plan that must be preserved because of constrained maneuverability, an emergency, a mission-critical task, or assigned operational priority. The framework predicts uncertainty-aware, well-clear separation violations with surrounding traffic and, when the priority plan alone cannot maintain separation, generates vehicle-constrained unilateral advisories that modify nearby aircraft trajectories to maintain well-clear separation for all traffic. The approach is applicable to any declared priority trajectory. This paper demonstrates the Plan component using a contingency landing planner to generate candidate priority trajectories. PAA then identifies nearby aircraft passing too close to this priority trajectory and issues Avoid resolution advisories to these aircraft. The framework is tested using real-world Automatic Dependent Surveillance-Broadcast (ADS-B) traffic from the Washington, D.C., airspace across more than 900 forced-landing cases, totaling over 140 hours of simulated flight. The PAA framework generates feasible cooperative advisories for all 575 unique conflict encounters, with a worst-case end-to-end response time of 5.7 s on a personal computer, including priority trajectory planning, advisory generation, and 1 s two-way datalink delay. In total, 93.5% of generated advisories satisfy the 35 s RTCA DO-365 Detect-and-Avoid temporal threshold. These results demonstrate low-latency coordination for preserving priority trajectories while maintaining well-clear separation through real-time automated advisory generation. Future work will quantify advisory-induced delays and their operational impacts.

</details>


### [164] [SoRoMoX: Fast, Differentiable, and Parallelizable Soft Robot Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06650)
*Maximilian Stölzle,Solange Gribonval,Daniel Feliu-Talegon,Vito Daniele Perfetta,Michele Martini,Chuhan Zhang,Kiwan Wong,Mohammed Tarnini,Anup Teejo Mathew,Federico Renda,Daniela Rus,Cosimo Della Santina*

Abstract: Reduced-order models based on Cosserat-rod theory are now well established, and modeling theory is no longer the primary bottleneck in soft-robot control. Their implementations, however, do not support the differentiable, GPU-parallel, and control-oriented workflows that underpin advanced rigid-robotics applications. Here, we fill this gap with SoRoMoX (Soft Robot Models in JAX), a fully numerical, JIT-compilable Python/JAX framework. SoRoMoX implements articulated, Piecewise Constant Strain, and Variable Strain models through a unified, control-ready interface that provides inertia matrices, gravitational and elastic forces, Jacobians, and their derivatives. To our knowledge, it is the first rod/strain-based soft-robot modeling framework that runs directly on GPUs and is end-to-end differentiable with respect to states, inputs, and parameters. Sequential CPU rollouts are up to 18.1x faster than state-of-the-art alternatives, while GPU-parallel rollouts increase throughput by up to 234.6x. This performance enables workflows that were previously impractical or impossible: static-equilibrium system identification with 66% lower marker RMSE; residual-force learning with a further 64% reduction; computed-torque tracking with RMSE reduced by a factor of approximately 500 relative to model-free PD; control-gain optimization with up to 62% lower loss than untuned gains; safety-constrained control using high-order control barrier functions to keep the peak contact force within a prescribed 5 N bound, compared with 33.5 N without the safety constraint; and reinforcement-learning policy training up to 7x faster than a CPU PyElastica discrete-rod baseline through massively parallel rollouts.

</details>


### [165] [CrossTracer: Cross-Embodiment Navigation via VLA Model Reasoning and Trace Residuals Adapting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06688)
*Yao Wang,Siyuan Wang,Zhirui Sun,Wenzheng Chi,Liang Lin,Jiankun Wang,Wenjun Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CrossTracer，一种面向跨形态机器人导航的分层框架，通过自适应轨迹残差将VLA模型的语义先验与具体机器人的运动约束对齐。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于机器人导航（robot navigation）和跨形态运动规划（cross-embodiment navigation），与遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等方向完全无关。不涉及任何遥感场景理解或卫星平台上的目标检测任务。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议深入阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人导航轨迹生成（navigation trace generation）；应用场景：轮式/腿式机器人室内外导航；传感器类型：第一人称视觉（egocentric observations）；目标类别：无目标检测，涉及可通行区域（traversability）和语义分割（panoptic segmentation）。

Model architecture: 分层框架：1) VL-Tracer：基于预训练VLA模型预测初始导航轨迹；2) CE-Adapter：基于可通行性视觉线索、机器人身份和初始轨迹预测残差修正；3) CE-RRT*：将全景分割转换为机器人条件可通行代价图，生成代价最小化像素空间轨迹。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署指标。仅在真实轮式和腿式机器人上进行了部署验证，但未给出具体计算资源需求。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：NaviTrace benchmark（跨形态导航轨迹生成基准）；评价指标：total score（综合得分），对比了Gemini-2.5-Pro等通用基线。

Experiments: CrossTracer在NaviTrace上总分45.68，超过Gemini-2.5-Pro 10.01分（相对提升28.1%）；真实机器人部署验证了导航成功率和执行效率的提升。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2) 未涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署技术；3) 方法依赖VLA大模型，计算开销可能较大，不适合星上资源受限场景；4) 仅在导航任务上验证，不涉及检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。但以下思路可迁移：1) "残差修正"（residual correction）思想——在遥感目标检测中，可用轻量网络对粗检测结果进行残差精调，平衡精度与速度；2) "统一像素空间接口"（unified pixel-space interface）——在星上多传感器融合中可借鉴统一表示层设计；3) CE-RRT*的代价图生成思路——可用于遥感图像中基于可通行性/可检测性的路径规划或感兴趣区域筛选。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide strong semantic priors for robot navigation, but they often ignore embodiment-specific mobility constraints. A path that is semantically plausible for one robot may be physically infeasible for another. We propose CrossTracer, a hierarchical framework for cross-embodiment navigation through adaptive trace residuals. CrossTracer represents navigation plans as normalized image-plane waypoints, forming a unified pixel-space interface between semantic reasoning and physical grounding. First, Vision-Language Trace Proposer (VL-Tracer) adapts a pretrained VLA model to predict an initial navigation trace from egocentric observations and flexible goal specifications. Second, CE-Adapter refines this trace by predicting embodiment-conditioned residual corrections from visual traversability cues, robot identity, and the initial trace. To train the refinement module without costly manual annotation, Cross-Embodiment RRT* (CE-RRT*) converts panoptic segmentation into robot-conditioned traversability cost maps and generates cost-minimizing pixel-space traces. We evaluate CrossTracer on the NaviTrace benchmark, which tests whether a model can generate embodiment-consistent navigation traces from egocentric observations, language instructions, and robot embodiment types. CrossTracer achieves a total score of 45.68, outperforming the strongest evaluated general-purpose baseline, Gemini-2.5-Pro, by 10.01 points, corresponding to a 28.1% relative improvement. Real-world deployment on wheeled and legged robots further shows improved navigation success and execution efficiency.

</details>

Experiments: 进行了涵道几何参数（duct、intake、nozzle）的扫描测试，对比了不同配置下的地面效应和自由飞行性能。优化配置在地面效应中升力提升近3倍，飞行时间增加60%；自由飞行中飞行时间减少30%。使用模式切换控制器演示了空中飞行、近地悬停和悬停-飞行转换。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何可借鉴的深度学习或计算机视觉方法。论文本身局限性包括：涵道附加质量降低了自由飞行的控制权限；自由飞行续航下降30%。

Abstract: Small rotorcraft intended for use indoors or around the built environment have extremely limited flight duration. This paper presents the design and experimental characterization of a custom shroud system that transforms a Crazyflie 2.1 micro air vehicle into a multi-modal robot capable of operating as a high-efficiency hovercraft or a free-flying drone. A custom experimental platform was developed for precise control of hover height and rotor duty cycle, and automated data logging of lift forces. Parametric testing of duct, intake, and nozzle geometries was performed to investigate the impact of shroud configuration on in-ground-effect and free-flight performance. An empirical model is developed which, unlike typical models for ground effect in rotorcraft, captures the suckdown effect that reduces force at intermediate height. It is shown that, through proper design of the shroud, beneficial ground effects can be increased while diminishing negative effects both close to the ground and in free flight. An optimized configuration exhibited nearly three times higher in-ground-effect force while maintaining comparable out-of-ground-effect aerodynamic thrust, although the added shroud mass reduces free-flight control authority. Lightweight shrouds are manufactured using thin-film thermoformed components, and total single-charge flight time is shown to increase by 60% in-ground-effect while decreasing by only 30% in free-flight as compared to the stock drone. Finally, controlled flight in the air, hovering close to the ground, and hover-to-flight transitions are demonstrated using a simple mode-switching controller, with tracking errors reported to quantify performance. This work provides an experimentally-validated and easily adoptable foundation for future research into lightweight ground-effect vehicles and hybrid drone-hovercraft systems.

</details>


### [167] [AtlasVLA: Persistent World-Ego State Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06729)
*Guiyu Zhao,Longteng Guo,Yanghong Mei,Zilin Zhu,Yu Zhang,Bin Cao,Mingming Yu,Xingjian He,Jie Jiang,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have advanced embodied AI, their fundamentally reactive paradigm severely limits performance in partially observable and long-horizon tasks. When restricted to a single wrist-mounted camera, they inevitably suffer from perception forgetting as objects exit the field of view, and temporal task-progress forgetting} during multi-step execution. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose AtlasVLA, a novel framework that transitions from direct reactive manipulation to proactive reasoning through a persistent world-ego state. AtlasVLA features a dual-memory architecture: a 4D Persistent World State Memory that lifts transient 2D observations into a globally updated, voxel-hashed spatial state to resolve visual blind spots, and an Ego-Working State Memory that tracks historical ego state and task progress. By conditioning a diffusion transformer (DiT) on this joint World-Ego state, AtlasVLA enables robust spatial reasoning. Extensive evaluations across LIBERO, RLBench, and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that AtlasVLA achieves state-of-the-art performance using solely a wrist camera. Remarkably, it decisively outperforms multi-view baselines, yielding absolute success rate improvements of 9.4% on LIBERO-Long and 17.5% in real-world long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [168] [Is Forward Prediction Enough? Physical State Grounding for JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06799)
*Haodong Yan,Jiaguan Zhu,Mingyuan Jia,Ruiqing Yin,Junjie He,Zhide Zhong,Junfeng Li,Jinxuan Lu,Hengtao Li,Tianran Zhang,Jiayi Chen,Wenxuan Song,Wen Chen,Yuxiang Gao,Haoang Li*

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦机器人世界模型（world model）的潜空间表示学习，属于机器人学/具身智能领域，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署均无任何关联。不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测任务或模型压缩方法。
