Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge graphs (KGs) accelerate drug discovery, but standard pipelines assume query molecules already exist as graph entities, leaving unregistered molecules disconnected. We address this cold-start challenge, termed the out-of-graph molecule problem, by introducing MolBioKG. This two-layer system grounds unseen molecules in biomedical evidence via multi-resolution structural anchoring. It connects an index of 2.74 million molecules (represented by scaffolds, fragments, functional groups, and fingerprints) to a 9.6-million-edge KG. Given only a SMILES string, MolBioKG retrieves structurally related graph entities and traverses their biomedical neighborhoods without task-specific training. It features two inference mechanisms: static multi-anchor retrieval using Reciprocal Rank Fusion, and Adapt-KG, a tool-using LLM policy for adaptive traversal. Evaluated across in-graph link recovery, complex multi-hop reasoning, and out-of-graph generalization, MolBioKG outperforms strong baselines. Notably, it raises Hits@10 from 0.585 to 0.876 in multi-hop reasoning and out-of-graph target recall from 0.145 to 0.269, all while ensuring predictions retain traceable structural anchors and source-attributed KG evidence.

</details>


### [223] [The Optimizer Is the Agent: Reasoning-Driven Search across Prompts, Programs, and ML Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06714)
*Junbo Li,Boyi Liu,Canwen Xu,Yite Wang,Yuxiong He,Zhangyang Wang,Qiang Liu,Zhewei Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReASearch框架，将提示词/程序/ML工作流的优化过程内化为单一工具型智能体的推理驱动搜索，而非依赖外部显式控制器。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化均无直接或间接关系。论文聚焦于通用AI优化框架（prompt/program/ML workflow优化），属于大语言模型智能体与自动化机器学习方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：提示词优化、程序优化、ML工作流优化。应用场景：通用AI系统自动调优。传感器类型：无。目标类别：无。与遥感场景无关。

Model architecture: ReASearch框架：基于工具型智能体（tool-using agent）的统一推理驱动优化框架，包含持久记忆（persistent memory）、自主决策（评估、诊断、编辑、验证/重启）循环。非目标检测模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 14个多样化任务（具体数据集名称摘要未说明）。评价指标：与领域专用优化基线对比，增益2%-40%。

Experiments: 在14个任务上与专用优化系统对比，ReASearch具有竞争力且多数优于基线，部分任务超越此前人类最佳结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 未讨论计算效率、模型压缩、边缘部署等关键问题；3. 依赖大语言模型作为智能体，推理开销可能较大，不适合资源受限的星上场景。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于LLM智能体优化方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交集。不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Recent systems for optimizing prompts, programs, and ML workflows typically rely on explicit outer-loop controllers such as evolutionary search, bandits, or textual-gradient methods. We ask a fundamentally different question: how much of this search policy can be internalized by a single tool-using agent? We present ReASearch, a unified framework for reasoning-driven optimization in which the agent autonomously decides what to evaluate, how to diagnose failures, which edits to make, and when to verify or restart. Rather than serving only as a proposal generator guided by hand-designed heuristics, the agent actively analyzes outcomes, allocates budget, and refines its strategy over long horizons through persistent memory. With a shared agent loop and domain-specific tools, ReASearch instantiates the exact same scaffold to optimize prompts, programs, and ML workflows. Across 14 diverse tasks, it is competitive with and mostly better than specialized optimization systems, achieving gains of 2% to 40% over strong domain-specific baselines, and in some cases discovering solutions that improve on prior human best-known results. Crucially, we observe that complex search behaviors, which are typically implemented by explicit controllers, emerge naturally from the agent's reasoning process.

</details>


### [224] [bioMoR: Biology-Guided Mixture-of-Recursions for Effective Genomic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06727)
*Koushik Howlader,Tirtho Roy,Md Tauhidul Islam,Wei Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出bioMoR，将Mixture-of-Recursions（MoR）自适应递归路由机制应用于基因/通路级组学数据分析，通过图结构知识引导token嵌入和递归深度分配，在提升精度的同时大幅降低参数量和FLOPs。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文面向生物组学（omics）数据分析，处理基因和通路，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然其提出的MoR自适应路由和轻量化思路（减少参数量75%、FLOPs降低58%）在模型压缩层面有方法论上的参考价值，但应用领域和任务类型与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议优先阅读。仅当对自适应计算分配（adaptive computation routing）这一通用轻量化范式感兴趣时，可作为跨领域参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：组学数据分析（基因级/通路级分类与特征选择）；应用场景：生物信息学、基因组学；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：基因、通路（非视觉目标）。

Model architecture: 基于Transformer的MoR（Mixture-of-Recursions）架构，引入图结构生物知识：1）图信息共享优化token嵌入；2）结构偏置引导自注意力聚焦生物相关token；3）图感知路由器利用邻域信息决定每个token的递归深度。

Lightweight method: MoR自适应递归路由机制：通过token-choice或expert-choice路由，仅对需要深度计算的token/通路进行递归计算，避免对所有token进行相同深度的全量计算。相比非递归Transformer，参数量减少75%，FLOPs降低58%。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。仅提及参数量减少75%、FLOPs降低58%，但未给出具体参数量数值、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。且该工作面向生物组学而非遥感，不涉及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等边缘部署工具链。

Datasets and metrics: 8个组学数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：macro-F1、balanced accuracy；实验协议：统一的五折交叉验证。

Experiments: 在8个组学基准上，bioMoR相比最强的biology-agnostic MoR基线，平均macro-F1提升8.2个百分点，balanced accuracy提升7.1个百分点；同时参数量减少75%、FLOPs降低58%（与非递归Transformer对比）。

Limitations: 1）与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，领域差异巨大；2）摘要未说明具体参数量、FLOPs绝对值、推理延迟等可迁移的量化指标；3）MoR路由机制在视觉任务（尤其是遥感目标检测）上的适用性未经验证；4）未涉及遥感特有的旋转框检测、小目标检测、多尺度问题。

Ideas for my research: 虽然领域不相关，但MoR的自适应递归路由思想有方法论借鉴意义：1）可探索将类似"token-wise自适应计算深度"机制引入遥感目标检测骨干网络，对简单背景区域减少计算量、对复杂目标区域增加计算量；2）图感知路由器的邻域信息聚合思路，可类比遥感图像中目标的空间上下文关系建模；3）"仅对需要深度计算的token进行递归"的思路，可启发遥感检测中的动态推理/early exit策略。但需注意，这些只是方法论层面的类比，直接迁移需要大量适配工作。

Abstract: Transformer models for high-dimensional omics analysis process thousands of genes or pathways, although only a subset requires deep computation. Mixture-of-Recursions (MoR) improves efficiency through adaptive token-choice or expert-choice routing. We propose bioMoR, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first framework to apply MoR to gene-level and pathway-level learning. Our contributions include identifying three locations for integrating structured biological knowledge within an MoR backbone: graph-based information sharing refines token embeddings, a structural bias guides self-attention toward biologically related tokens, and a graph-aware router uses neighborhood information to determine each token's recursion depth. These techniques are centered on our insight that additional knowledge of token interaction can effectively help models construct embeddings and select which tokens should be learned more deeply. Across eight benchmarks spanning diverse omics data types and evaluated under a unified five-fold cross-validation protocol, bioMoR improves average macro-F1 by 8.2 percentage points and balanced accuracy by 7.1 percentage points over the strongest biology-agnostic MoR baseline while using 75 percent fewer parameters and up to 58 percent fewer FLOPs than a non-recursive Transformer. The selected marker genes or pathways provide biological interpretability, while their token-specific recursion depths reveal how computation is allocated.

</details>


### [225] [From Cheap Fakes to Pure Synthesis: Addressing the New Era of T2V Fake News Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06732)
*Yifeng Luo,Yupeng Li,Liang Lan,Tian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个纯合成假新闻视频数据集PS-FNVD，并设计R-T2V框架进行三元假新闻视频检测，与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是假新闻视频检测（fake news video detection），属于多模态内容安全领域，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。不涉及遥感图像、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：假新闻视频三元分类（真实/廉价伪造/纯合成伪造）；场景：新闻视频内容安全；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: R-T2V框架，结合条件推理生成（conditioned rationale generation）和监督微调（supervised fine-tuning），融合高层语义逻辑与低层物理生成痕迹进行三元真实性标签预测。具体网络结构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：PS-FNVD（首个纯合成假新闻视频数据集，包含两类伪造事件）；评价指标：Accuracy、macro F₁；对比方法：10个基线模型。

Experiments: R-T2V在10个基线模型上达到SOTA，准确率比第二名高12.20个百分点，macro F₁高8.46个百分点。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或遥感检测技术信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究的是假新闻视频检测，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等任何与本课题相关的技术方向。

Abstract: Recent text-to-video (T2V) generation models enable fake news videos to be synthesized from scratch, shifting the threat beyond cheap fakes assembled from existing footage. Such news videos can closely match fabricated narratives, creating a modality alignment trap for existing detectors. Existing datasets lack pure synthesis fake news videos. Although directly prompting T2V models with descriptions of fake news videos can yield perfectly aligned samples, it reduces the fake news video detection (FNVD) to unimodal shortcuts and causes semantic-visual degeneration. To counter this, we formulate T2V-FNVD as a novel ternary classification task with three labels (real, cheap fake, and pure synthesis fake) and construct the first pure synthesis fake news video dataset (PS-FNVD). PS-FNVD includes fabricated events with aligned deception (Type 1) and true events with false visual provenance (Type 2), preventing models from exploiting unimodal shortcuts. Furthermore, we propose the Reasoning-guided T2V-FNVD (R-T2V) framework. Trained through conditioned rationale generation and supervised fine-tuning, R-T2V integrates high-level semantic logic with low-level physical generative traces to predict the ternary veracity label. Extensive experiments across 10 prevailing baselines show that R-T2V achieves the state-of-the-art performance, outperforming the second-best baseline by 12.20 percentage points in accuracy and 8.46 percentage points in macro $F_1$.

</details>


### [226] [IB-RL: Isolated Bilateral Reinforcement Learning for Strategic Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06735)
*Senhao Wang,Chenghao Cai,Haitao Hu,Mingxing Huang,Xingguang Wang,Wenhao Li,Zecheng Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出IB-RL方法，通过双边独立强化学习解决对话策略对固定对手过拟合的问题，提升跨对手泛化能力。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于自然语言处理中的强化学习对话策略研究，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：强化学习对话策略泛化；应用场景：Vehicle TeleSales（车辆电话销售）和Deal-or-NoDeal（谈判游戏）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及使用RL训练LLM，以及IB-RL框架（双边独立优势函数、动作掩码、更新路径）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Vehicle TeleSales、Deal-or-NoDeal（对话/谈判数据集）；评价指标：Success@1（车辆销售）、agreement rate（谈判）。

Experiments: IB-RL在Vehicle TeleSales上达到89.6% Success@1（最优单边RL基线84.6%）；在Deal-or-NoDeal上对DeepSeek V4 Pro达到98.4% agreement（最优单边基线86.4%）。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。与遥感目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术点。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文研究的是LLM对话策略的强化学习泛化问题，与遥感在轨目标检测的模型轻量化、部署优化等核心问题无任何交集。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved strong results in improving large language models (LLMs) on tasks with stationary, verifiable rewards, such as mathematical reasoning and code execution. In these settings, the environment follows fixed rules and does not adapt strategically to the agent. Strategic dialogue differs in this respect: the environment is another agent that adapts to the policy, and success depends on the interaction between the two sides. Despite this interactive nature, current RL approaches typically train a target agent against a fixed counterpart or simulator. We find that this training paradigm encourages the policy to exploit counterpart-specific regularities rather than learn strategies that generalize across counterparts. We call this problem the static-counterpart mismatch, which we quantify directly in our experiments. To address it, we propose Isolated Bilateral Reinforcement Learning (IB-RL), in which the two roles coevolve through joint rollouts while each role optimizes its own reward through fully independent advantages, action masks, and update paths. We evaluate frozen policies against fully independent held-out counterparts in both domains. On Vehicle TeleSales, IB-RL achieves 89.6% Success@1, compared to 84.6% for the best unilateral RL baseline. On Deal-or-NoDeal, it reaches 98.4% agreement against DeepSeek V4 Pro, compared to 86.4% for the best unilateral baseline. These results indicate that jointly training both roles with strict peragent isolation produces policies that generalize more effectively to unseen counterparts.

</details>


### [227] [MemPrism: Task-Conditioned Relational Memory Views for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06745)
*Zhisheng Chen,Bingfan Zeng,Bangde Cao,Zhengwei Xie,Yuxuan Li,Jinhan Li,Zheng Lu,Xiangchen Guan,Zikai Xiao,Rui Qian,Jingwei Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MemPrism，一种任务条件关系记忆框架，通过动态构建关系视图来缓解长时程智能体中的表征不匹配问题，提升任务性能并降低记忆token消耗。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是长时程智能体（long-horizon agents）的记忆系统，属于具身智能和Web智能体领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。摘要中未出现任何遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化相关术语。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文研究的是智能体记忆系统，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：长时程智能体记忆管理；应用场景：具身智能（embodied agents）和Web智能体（web-agent）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: MemPrism框架包含：持久化事件流存储（persistent experience storage）、任务条件关系视图构建器（task-conditioned relational view）、轻量级视图策略（lightweight view policy）、确定性组合器（deterministic composer）和渲染器（render），将历史事实转换为临时的光学工作记忆视图（optical working-memory view）供冻结的任务策略使用。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到"lightweight view policy"（轻量级视图策略），但这是针对记忆视图选择策略的轻量化设计，并非模型压缩或加速方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。与遥感模型轻量化无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"reducing memory token consumption"（降低记忆token消耗），但这是针对LLM/VLM推理的token开销，而非星上部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 实验在长时程具身智能和Web智能体基准（long-horizon embodied and web-agent benchmarks）上进行，具体数据集名称摘要未说明。评价指标为任务性能（task performance）和记忆token消耗（memory token consumption）。

Experiments: 实验结果表明：MemPrism一致提升了任务性能，尤其在轨迹变长时效果更明显；同时降低了记忆token消耗。学习的视图策略可在不同VLM间迁移，无需额外适配。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。与遥感在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，无直接可借鉴点。其"任务条件关系视图"（task-conditioned relational view）思想在通用AI记忆系统中有意义，但不适用于遥感目标检测的轻量化场景。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents rely on memory to reuse experiences, yet existing memory systems often assume that evidence can be directly consumed through a fixed representation. This leads to representation mismatch, where relevant information is available but not organized for the current decision. To this end, we propose MemPrism, a task-conditioned relational memory framework that separates persistent experience storage from decision-time working memory. MemPrism records interactions as the event stream and dynamically constructs relational views according to the current task context. A lightweight view policy selects the relation structure, evidence range, outcome condition, and granularity, while a deterministic composer and render transform historical facts into a temporary optical working-memory view for a frozen task policy. Experiments on long-horizon embodied and web-agent benchmarks show that MemPrism consistently improves the task performance, especially as trajectories become longer, while reducing memory token consumption. Furthermore, the learned view policy transfers across different VLMs without additional adaptation, demonstrating the effectiveness of task-conditioned relational views as a general memory interface for agents.

</details>


### [228] [Mind the Gap: A Dual Knowledge Graph Framework for Unified Multi-task User Intent Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06752)
*Tzu-Cheng Peng,Chien Chin Chen,Chih-Hao Ku,Yung-Chun Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 DKG-MTI，一个基于双知识图谱的框架，用于从在线旅游评论中统一进行多任务用户意图推断（方面评分预测 + 反向意图语句生成），通过结构感知语义平滑对齐用户特定意图知识图谱与全局酒店知识图谱，并交由大语言模型处理。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2 分）。该论文属于自然语言处理/推荐系统领域，处理的是在线旅游评论（TripAdvisor）的用户意图推断，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测或遥感场景理解；也不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT 等任何模型压缩或部署技术。核心是知识图谱 + LLM 的意图推断，属于泛 NLP 方法，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1 分。论文主题为 NLP 用户意图推断，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，无直接借鉴价值，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多任务用户意图推断（aspect rating 分类 + reverse user intent 生成）。场景：在线旅游评论（TripAdvisor）。传感器类型：不适用（文本数据）。目标类别：用户意图、方面评分，非视觉目标。

Model architecture: DKG-MTI 框架：动态构建 User-Specific Intent Knowledge Graph，与 Global Hotel Knowledge Graph 通过 structure-aware semantic smoothing 对齐，对齐后的知识与原始评论结合，交由 large language model (LLM) 同时预测方面评分并生成反向用户意图语句。属于 inference-only 知识增强框架。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性指标。且其依赖 LLM，通常计算开销大，与在轨轻量化部署方向相悖。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：TripAdvisor 在线旅游评论。指标：分类任务与意图生成任务的性能指标（具体指标名称摘要未说明），对比基线为强 LLM 与 retrieval-based 方法。

Experiments: 在 TripAdvisor 评论上，DKG-MTI 在分类和意图生成任务上均优于强 LLM 与 retrieval-based 基线，验证了 structure-aware knowledge alignment 对可扩展、可解释意图推断的有效性。具体数值摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供遥感/目标检测/轻量化方面的信息。摘要未说明模型规模、推理成本、部署可行性；依赖 LLM 导致计算开销大，不适合资源受限场景。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。唯一可迁移的抽象思想是"知识图谱结构感知对齐"用于增强模型推理，但该思路面向 NLP 意图推断，与遥感在轨目标检测的视觉特征、轻量化约束差异巨大。若强行迁移，可考虑将领域先验知识（如遥感目标的空间关系、类别共现结构）以图结构注入轻量检测模型，但需自行设计，论文本身未提供相关技术细节。

Abstract: This paper proposes DKG-MTI, a dual knowledge graph framework for unified multi-task user intent inference from online travel reviews. Existing approaches often rely on hierarchical pipelines that suffer from error propagation or retrieval methods that ignore structural relationships in domain knowledge. To address these limitations, we introduce an inference-only knowledge augmentation framework that dynamically constructs a User-Specific Intent Knowledge Graph from each review and aligns it with a Global Hotel Knowledge Graph through structure-aware semantic smoothing. The aligned knowledge is combined with the original review and processed by a large language model to simultaneously predict aspect ratings and generate reverse user intent statements. Experiments on TripAdvisor reviews show that DKG-MTI consistently outperforms strong LLM and retrieval-based baselines in both classification and intent generation tasks, demonstrating the effectiveness of structure-aware knowledge alignment for scalable and explainable intent inference.

</details>


### [229] [Capek 0.5: An Execution-Centric Vision-Language Model for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06756)
*Ying Chen,Weizhen Li,Zhe Hu,Zhenjiang Li,Rui Jiang,Zhifeng Gu,Lihuang Fang,Jiangping Liu,Lei Yi,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Capek 0.5，一个面向具身智能体的执行中心能力分类视觉-语言模型，通过强化学习训练专用专家并通过权重合并与路由策略蒸馏整合为统一模型。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于具身智能体(embodied agent)的视觉-语言模型，涉及机器人执行中的空间推理、时间理解、动作引导和状态验证，属于机器人/具身AI领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感核心概念，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：具身视觉-语言推理与执行；应用场景：机器人/具身智能体执行；传感器类型：视觉(通用)；目标类别：无特定目标检测类别。不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: Capek 0.5，基于共享backbone的视觉-语言模型，包含四个能力专家(Spatial Reasoning, Temporal Understanding, Action Guidance, State Verification)，通过权重空间合并(weight-space merging)和路由策略蒸馏(routed policy-space distillation)整合。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏(除策略蒸馏外)、NAS等轻量化方法。提到的"weight-space merging"和"routed policy-space distillation"主要用于能力整合而非模型压缩。模型规模为2B和35B-A3B(可能指35B参数但激活3B)，但未从轻量化角度讨论。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量(除2B/35B-A3B规模外)、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。未涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 提出Capek-StateBench新基准用于状态验证；使用综合benchmark套件评估；进行受控能力保留研究和闭环仿真环境评估。具体数据集名称摘要未详细列出。

Experiments: Capek 0.5在大多数匹配的benchmark行上优于初始化模型；在一个checkpoint中保留了所有四种专门化能力(有量化损失)；可迁移到闭环具身任务执行。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法提供该方向的技术参考。摘要未说明模型在遥感数据上的表现、目标检测能力、轻量化部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。可借鉴的点非常有限：1) 能力分类(能力族)的思想或许可用于组织遥感在轨检测的不同子任务(如检测、定位、验证)；2) 权重空间合并和策略蒸馏作为多任务整合方法，在遥感多任务轻量模型中可能有参考价值，但需要大幅改造。整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴意义很小。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly serving as the reasoning core of embodied agents. Robot execution is inherently iterative: each action reshapes the scene and physical state, continually renewing what must be perceived, reasoned about, and verified. Meeting these demands requires complementary capabilities that differ in supervision signals, prediction formats, and verification criteria. Existing approaches typically develop these capabilities against isolated, task-specific objectives, leaving open how they should be organized and integrated around execution as a whole. We present Capek 0.5, an embodied vision-language model built around an execution-centric capability taxonomy. Rather than organizing training by datasets or tasks, the taxonomy groups embodied capabilities according to their functional roles throughout execution and comprises four capability families: Spatial Reasoning, Temporal Understanding, Action Guidance, and State Verification. Each capability is first acquired by a dedicated specialist through reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards from a shared backbone, and the specialists are then consolidated into a single inference-time model through weight-space merging followed by routed policy-space distillation. We instantiate Capek 0.5 at the 2B and 35B-A3B scales and evaluate it from three complementary perspectives: comprehensive benchmark suites including Capek-StateBench, a new benchmark for state verification; a controlled study of capability retention from specialists to the unified model; and closed-loop evaluation in simulated embodied environments. Capek 0.5 improves the large majority of matched benchmark rows over its initialization, retains all four specialized capabilities in one checkpoint with quantified losses, and transfers to closed-loop embodied task execution.

</details>


### [230] [LiFTER: A Grounded Neuro-Symbolic Microscope for Continuous-Time Dynamic Graph Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06765)
*Minwoo Yu,Young-guk Ha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号时序规则归纳器LiFTER，将连续时间动态图上的未来链接预测转化为可验证的接地计算，实现可解释的预测。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署完全无关。它研究的是连续时间动态图（CTDG）上的未来链接预测问题，属于图神经网络/知识图谱/可解释AI领域，不涉及任何遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：连续时间动态图上的未来链接预测（future-link forecasting）；应用场景：时序知识图谱推理；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: LiFTER（Link-Fact Temporal Rule Inducer）：神经符号预测器，将观测到的交互作为接地时序事实保存，并应用可执行的时序规则对预查询事实进行推理。每个分数是规则执行的带符号和，其中历史事实、实体绑定和时序顺序被显式满足。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：4个CTDG基准数据集；评价指标：历史负向预测（historical-negative forecasting）性能、宏观解释准确率（macro explanation accuracy）、删除保真度（deletion fidelity）。

Experiments: 在4个CTDG基准上，LiFTER达到有竞争力的历史负向预测性能，以及最高的宏观解释准确率和删除保真度。独立执行可重构19664个测试预测的所有logits，最大误差0.0000131。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型压缩内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究的是时序图上的链接预测可解释性，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在任务、数据、方法上均无交集。

Abstract: Continuous-time dynamic graph models predict future links by compressing past interactions into neural states. Although effective for forecasting, this computation obscures which entities are shared across events and how temporal patterns contribute to a prediction. We treat this gap as a property of the predictive architecture rather than a problem to be addressed after prediction. Link-Fact Temporal Rule Inducer (LiFTER) is a neuro-symbolic predictor that preserves observed interactions as grounded temporal facts and applies executable tempo- ral rules to pre-query facts. Each score is a signed sum of rule exe- cutions whose historical facts, entity bindings, and temporal order are explicitly satisfied. The evidence and rules responsible for a prediction can therefore be inspected, independently recomputed, and intervened upon. Across four CTDG benchmarks, LiFTER achieves competitive historical-negative forecasting and the highest macro explanation ac- curacy and deletion fidelity. The same architecture also serves as a microscope that separates the contributions of recurrence, history po- sition, and transition across datasets and traces them to individual facts. Independent execution reconstructs all logits for 19,664 test predictions with a maximum error of 0.0000131. LiFTER turns future-link forecasting into a verifiable grounded computation.

</details>


### [231] [Surg-UniWorld: A Unified Surgical World Model with Multimodal Control Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06770)
*Rulin Zhou,Wanhao Liu,Guoheng Ma,Liangjin Shao,Qiujie Song,Yidu Wang,Guankun Wang,Tong Chen,Long Bai,Luping Zhou,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Surg-UniWorld，一个面向手术场景的多模态可控世界模型，用于生成逼真的器械-组织交互视频，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于手术机器人领域的手术视频生成（surgical world model），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感相关主题；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、边缘部署等模型压缩或加速方法。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：可控手术视频生成（controllable surgical video generation）；应用场景：手术人工智能与仿真（surgical AI and simulation）；传感器类型：手术内窥镜视频；目标类别：手术器械与组织（instrument-tissue interactions）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: Surg-UniWorld：基于Hierarchical Surgical Anchor（层次化手术锚点）和Anchor-Relative Modality Experts（锚点相对模态专家）的多模态控制框架，使用Wan2.2视频扩散骨干网络（video diffusion backbone）。包含边缘、深度、光流等多模态控制专家。与遥感目标检测模型无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计、实时推理或边缘部署。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该模型基于视频扩散模型（Wan2.2 backbone），计算开销极大，不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Cholec80-SurgWAM（自建手术视频生成基准）；评价指标：生成质量（generation quality）、时间一致性（temporal consistency）、多模态可控性（multimodal controllability）。与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: 实验表明Surg-UniWorld在生成质量、时间一致性和多模态可控性上优于现有可控视频生成方法和手术世界模型基线。摘要未提供具体数值对比。

Limitations: 1. 完全面向手术场景，与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化无任何交集；2. 基于视频扩散模型，计算量巨大，不具备轻量化或边缘部署可行性；3. 摘要未讨论模型效率或实时性。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人课题方向基本无关，无可借鉴点。但可注意：其"多模态控制专家"的设计思路（如锚点相对编码）在理论上可迁移至遥感多模态融合（如光学+SAR），但需大幅改造且与轻量化目标冲突，不建议直接借鉴。

Abstract: Controllable surgical world models can provide a generative foundation for surgical artificial intelligence and simulation by synthesizing realistic instrument--tissue interactions. However, existing methods lack a unified multimodal control paradigm, while direct fusion of heterogeneous visual conditions often causes anatomical distortion, instrument appearance drift, and temporally inconsistent interactions. In this work, we propose {Surg-UniWorld}, a unified surgical world model with multimodal control experts. Surg-UniWorld first constructs a {Hierarchical Surgical Anchor} from first-frame appearance and hierarchical semantic masks to preserve persistent scene identity, anatomical organization, and interaction boundaries. {Anchor-Relative Modality Experts} then interpret edge, depth, and optical-flow evidence relative to the shared anchor, capturing complementary boundary, geometric, and motion information. A {Multimodal Control Expert} further performs contribution-preserving stage-wise composition of the activated modality increments and generates control hints for the Wan2.2 video diffusion backbone. To support multimodal surgical world modeling, we further construct Cholec80-SurgWAM, a benchmark for controllable surgical video generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Surg-UniWorld consistently outperforms existing controllable video generation methods and surgical world-model baselines in generation quality, temporal consistency, and multimodal controllability.

</details>


### [232] [Evolving Parallel Algorithm Portfolios via Potential-Aware Instance Generation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06808)
*Shaofeng Zhang,Shengcai Liu,Zhiyuan Wang,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: The Automatic Construction of Portfolios via Large Language Models (LLM-ACP) suffers from poor generalization in practical few-shot scenarios when solving complex combinatorial optimization problems. Instance and algorithm co-evolution frameworks address this by expanding the training dataset with generated hard instances on which the current algorithm portfolio underperforms, thereby enhancing generalization. However, this paradigm faces two critical limitations: evaluating instance hardness relies on high-quality reference solutions, and single-mode generation patterns limit instance diversity. To overcome these limitations, we introduce the Potential-aware Instance and Algorithm Co-evolution (PIAC) framework. Our core contribution is twofold. First, we propose potential gain, a novel metric that eliminates the need for reference solutions. This metric estimates generalization gain by perturbing the generated algorithms and assessing their improvement potential on generated problem instances. Second, PIAC leverages LLMs to synthesize diverse instance mutators, exploring a broader region of the problem-instance space and thereby enhancing the portfolio's generalization capabilities. Given that perturbation spaces vary across different algorithms, we instantiate our framework on Greedy Constructive, Ant Colony Optimization, and Guided Local Search algorithmic backbones. Comprehensive evaluations on the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) and Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (CVRP) across six distinct data distributions demonstrate that PIAC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art LLM-ACP baselines, notably achieving a 19.76% relative improvement for TSP Greedy Constructive portfolios.

</details>


### [233] [Gated-BEPO: Confidence-Gated Bellman Credit Assignment for Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06861)
*Hongxi Yan,Ziyue Huang,Shichao Fan,Qingjie Liu*

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Training large language model agents in long-horizon environments requires assigning credit from sparse terminal outcomes to individual actions. Existing critic-free methods propagate trajectory-level rewards uniformly across steps, while recent approaches construct step-level groups by matching repeated states and compare actions within each group. The former cannot distinguish useful actions in failed trajectories from ineffective actions in successful ones. The latter rely on step credit derived directly from individual trajectory outcomes and fixed-weight fusion with episode-level credit. We propose Gated-BEPO, which derives step-level credit from empirical rollout graphs. For each rollout group, Gated-BEPO constructs an empirical graph and estimates node values through a mean-backup Bellman fixed point that reflects the empirical action distribution of the current policy. We then accumulate these temporal-difference residuals along each sampled trajectory using generalized advantage estimation, yielding step-level Bellman advantages that capture both immediate and downstream effects. To adaptively fuse episode- and step-level credit, a confidence gate incorporates Bellman credit only at states with multiple observed successors and otherwise uses episode-level credit. Experiments on WebShop, ALFWorld, and visual Sokoban show consistent improvements across language and vision-language models, while diagnostic ablations support the effectiveness of Bellman fixed-point value estimation and show that step-level credit should be incorporated selectively rather than uniformly into the final advantage.

</details>


### [234] [CEDAR: Agent-Orchestrated Tree Search for Goal-Directed Optimization of Complex Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06871)
*Yingtao Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CEDAR，一种利用大语言模型(LLM)驱动的蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)来自动发现满足用户指定行为目标的复杂系统的方法，与遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是人工生命(Artificial Life)中的复杂系统建模与自动发现，涉及LLM、MCTS、进化计算等，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化/部署等方向完全无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人研究方向基本无关，无可借鉴点。但若从方法论角度，LLM+MCTS的自动化设计思路或许可启发自动化网络架构搜索(NAS)中的搜索策略设计，但关联性极弱。

Abstract: Complex systems, core objects of study in artificial life, model diverse phenomena through nonlinear, feedback-driven interactions that produce emergent behavior, with applications from population dynamics and biology to economic policy and strategic decision-making. Yet the difficulty of predicting how feedback structure gives rise to emergent behavior, a central open problem in artificial life, makes goal-directed design exceptionally challenging. In established practice, system structures are written in specialized modeling languages such as DYNAMO or STELLA, compounding the challenge with labor-intensive workflows that limit adoption and hinder timely decision-making. To address these challenges, we introduce CEDAR, an autonomous method that uses Large Language Model (LLM) agents to discover complex systems satisfying user-specified behavioral goals. Our key innovation is an LLM-driven Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) deeply coupled with complex systems: at each iteration, an LLM Judge evaluates emergent behavior against specified goals and an LLM Editor proposes improved variants, with the Judge acting as a fitness function and the Editor as a variation operator, akin to a generate-and-evaluate loop in evolutionary computation. We represent complex systems as a restricted, runnable subset of Python with domain-specific primitives, letting LLMs modify system dynamics directly. CEDAR formalizes this as an MCTS variant with an LLM-parameterized transition kernel and value function, enabling goal-directed discovery of complex system behaviors while preserving solution diversity, and its LLM-based interpretability reveals how structural changes drive emergent behavior. CEDAR reduces human effort while enabling capabilities difficult to achieve with existing approaches, facilitating broader adoption of complex systems across domains.

</details>


### [235] [SkillEval: Decomposing Agent Skill Quality into Interpretable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06891)
*Jiahui Han,Qinuo Li,Ziheng Peng,Haotian Wu,Haoze Liu,Danfeng Shan,Guanchu Wang,Huiqi Deng,Ninghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Agent skills provide reusable procedural knowledge that helps agents solve specialized tasks. As their use expands, evaluating skill quality becomes increasingly important. Existing evaluations often measure skill quality by testing whether a skill improves performance on specific downstream tasks. However, a reusable skill may apply to multiple task scenarios. Downstream evaluation mainly reflects the compatibility between a skill and the evaluated task, provides only a partial view of skill quality, and does not identify which aspect of the skill should be improved. We find that general properties of the \texttt{SKILL.md} document play an important role in skill quality. To evaluate these properties, we propose \textbf{SkillEval}, an interpretable framework for document-level skill evaluation. SkillEval evaluates each property using a fixed and inspectable scoring direction, producing interpretable scores. It further measures and reduces the influence of unrelated document features, such as length and formatting, so that each score captures its intended semantic property more specifically. Specifically, SkillEval learns an interpretable direction for each quality property from controlled positive--negative skill pairs in the hidden representation space of the model, and scores a new skill by projecting its representation onto these fixed directions. We use SkillEval to evaluate skills in controlled quality tests and show that SkillEval reliably distinguishes skills of different quality. In addition, SkillEval scores closely reflect downstream task performance, providing an early indication of whether a skill is likely to help an agent complete a task. We further explore SkillEval for diagnosing weaknesses in skill documents and guiding targeted revisions. The revised skills improve the targeted properties and achieve higher pass rates on downstream tasks.

</details>


### [236] [From Points to Edges: Edge-Conditioned Spectral Operators for Physics-Sensitive PDE Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06894)
*Zhentao Tan,Ruijie Quan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Edge-Conditioned Spectral Operator (ESO)，通过局部边缘信息调制全局谱混合，提升偏微分方程求解精度。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全聚焦于偏微分方程(PDE)求解的神经算子方法，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无任何关联。属于科学计算/物理信息机器学习方向。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：PDE求解（Darcy flow等物理问题）；应用场景：科学计算/计算物理；传感器类型：无；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: Edge-Conditioned Spectral Operator (ESO)，包含Pairwise-Variation Modal Mixer (PVMM)和Physics-Aware Reweighting (PAR)模块。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向科学计算而非边缘/星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 9个PDE基准测试集；评价指标为求解误差（如相对误差、区域误差等）。

Experiments: 在9个PDE基准上达到SOTA；可视化分析显示在系数跳跃、高梯度流结构等物理敏感区域误差降低。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于科学计算领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Abstract: Neural operators have become a central tool for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), with spectral operators offering efficient global mixing across spatial locations. However, many PDEs contain physics-sensitive local structures that are critical to the underlying physical behavior. For example, in Darcy flow, local material interfaces are often reflected by sharp changes in the permeability field and can strongly influence the solution. Existing spectral operators primarily adapt modal mixing based on center-point representations, making them insufficiently responsive to such localized structural variations. We propose the Edge-Conditioned Spectral Operator (ESO), a novel spectral operator framework that modulates global spectral mixing using local edge-wise variations. By incorporating the Pairwise-Variation Modal Mixer (PVMM) to inject local edge information into spectral mode selection, ESO preserves the global approximation capability of spectral neural operators while enabling the learned kernel to adapt to physics-sensitive local structures. Furthermore, we introduce a task-adaptive Physics-Aware Reweighting (PAR) that emphasizes physically important regions, identified by taskspecific physical quantities. Across nine PDE benchmarks, ESO consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance. Visual and region-wise analyses further demonstrate that ESO reduces solution errors near coefficient jumps, high-gradient flow structures, and other physically sensitive regions. The code is available at https://github.com/Tanpig-X/ESO.

</details>


### [237] [Long-Horizon Agent Trajectory Attribution: A Unified Benchmark and Fine-Grained Annotation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06909)
*Jing Chen,Yang Sun,Li Zhang,Lin Xu,Jie Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究大语言模型智能体的长程轨迹归因分析，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于LLM Agent的轨迹归因（trajectory attribution）基准构建与评估，属于自然语言处理/智能体安全领域。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等任何检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何轻量化或部署技术。与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM Agent轨迹归因分析（trajectory attribution）。应用场景：智能体安全评估、可解释性。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。涉及LLM Agent的轨迹数据标准化、归因标注框架。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：AgentDojo轨迹、Agent3Sigma的Stage和Canary设置，共1300+条标注轨迹。评价指标：primary attribution localization（主归因定位）和attribution-chain recovery（归因链恢复）。

Experiments: 提供了基于增量轨迹贡献和组件级留一扰动的参考基线。基线结果在不同归因设置下表现出显著性能差异。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于LLM Agent安全与可解释性方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly operate through long-horizon trajectories involving user instructions, tool use, external observations, and memory. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate behavioral outcomes but provide limited support for fine-grained attribution analysis. We introduce trajectory attribution and develop a benchmark and annotation framework for this task. The benchmark organizes heterogeneous trajectories under a unified component schema and provides annotations of the primary attribution component, together with attack and execution chains where applicable. Instantiating the benchmark with trajectories from AgentDojo and the Stage and Canary settings of Agent3Sigma yields more than 1,300 annotated trajectories covering task-aligned actions, unsafe actions, and safety refusals. The benchmark defines two evaluation tasks, primary attribution localization and attribution-chain recovery, and provides reference baselines based on incremental trajectory contribution and component-level leave-one-out perturbation. It captures diverse attribution settings, including local and long-range attribution as well as structured attribution chains. Reference baseline results exhibit substantial performance differences across these settings, providing an initial characterization of the benchmark's attribution challenges. Beyond this initial instantiation, we release a reusable annotation skill that enables trajectories generated by new agent models to be standardized, annotated, and evaluated under the same framework. Project resources and future releases are available at https://github.com/chenjing-2024/agent-trajectory-attribution.

</details>


### [238] [WNM-3D: A World Navigation Model with 3D Scene Conditioning for Closed-Loop VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07267)
*Yuehao Huang,Yunzi Wu,Xiaotao Zhang,Xinhai Li,Jiankun Dong,Jiajun Lv,Chi Zhang,Chenjia Bai,Yong Liu,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出WNM-3D，一种基于3D场景条件生成式世界导航模型，用于连续视觉-语言导航任务，通过几何感知表示联合预测未来观测和动作。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究视觉-语言导航（VLN），属于具身智能/机器人导航领域，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等遥感目标检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等模型压缩或部署技术。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：连续视觉-语言导航（continuous VLN）；应用场景：室内/室外机器人导航；传感器类型：单目RGB相机；目标类别：无目标检测，关注导航动作和未来观测预测。

Model architecture: 核心架构：WNM-3D，包含冻结的前馈几何编码器（feed-forward geometry encoder）提取几何感知表示，可训练的3D场景到Token适配器（3D Scene-to-Token Adapter）将几何表示转换为固定长度前缀，世界动作扩散Transformer（world-action Diffusion Transformer）通过块因果注意力（block-causal attention）联合生成未来视频和动作。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。模型使用扩散Transformer，计算开销较大，不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：GN-Bench；评价指标：闭环导航性能（closed-loop navigation performance）、流动作一致性（flow-action consistency）、视觉-运动误差（visual-motion error）。

Experiments: 在GN-Bench上，WNM-3D优于强VLM导航策略及其2D条件版本；在固定近目标评估集上，实现了更高的流动作一致性和更低的视觉-运动误差。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；模型基于扩散Transformer，计算量大，不适合资源受限的星上场景；未提供任何轻量化或部署相关信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。但以下思路可抽象参考：(1) 几何感知表示（geometry-aware representation）的思想可用于遥感目标检测中利用3D几何信息增强检测；(2) 将历史观测压缩为固定长度前缀（fixed-length prefix）的token化方法，可类比于遥感时序数据压缩；(3) 生成式世界模型（world-action model）的思路与遥感视频预测/变化检测可能有交叉，但与本课题核心方向偏离较大。

Abstract: Recent vision-language navigation (VLN) systems increasingly adapt pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) into vision-language-action (VLA) policies that map egocentric observations and language instructions directly to navigation actions. Although semantically capable, such action-centric training does not explicitly model how the agent's visual observations should evolve under its predicted motion. Generative world-action models (WAMs) jointly predict future observations and actions, yet existing WAMs for continuous VLN do not condition joint future-view and action generation on geometry-aware representations inferred from the observed history. We present WNM-3D, a generative World Navigation Model with 3D scene conditioning for continuous VLN. To consolidate past observations into persistent scene context, a frozen feed-forward geometry encoder extracts geometry-aware representations from the monocular egocentric RGB history, and a trainable 3D Scene-to-Token Adapter converts them into a fixed-length prefix in the token space of the world-action Diffusion Transformer. Through block-causal attention, this prefix conditions every future video-action block, providing a shared geometric context for both future-view and action generation. We train WNM-3D through supervised world-action fine-tuning on A*-generated demonstrations, DAgger-style adaptation on policy-visited states, and DanceGRPO-based closed-loop policy optimization. Experiments on GN-Bench show that WNM-3D outperforms strong VLM-based navigation policies and its 2D-conditioned counterpart in closed-loop navigation. On a fixed near-goal evaluation set, WNM-3D also achieves higher flow-action consistency and lower visual-motion error.

</details>


### [239] [Fast LapSum: Exact Differentiable Top-k at Million Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06912)
*Łukasz Struski,Joanna Wojciechowicz,Jakub Antczak,Marcin Mazur,Kamil Książek,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Fast LapSum，一种精确预算的软top-k算子，在GPU上线性时间内实现完全可微的稀疏选择，用于大规模稀疏计算。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测及轻量化基本无关。论文核心贡献是提出一种新的可微软top-k算子（Fast LapSum），面向稀疏计算、token路由、专家激活、注意力剪枝等通用机器学习场景，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 通用稀疏计算任务，应用场景包括稀疏路由、检索、大规模优化、稀疏对抗样本生成、稀疏图像编码。不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: Fast LapSum是一种软top-k算子，结合线性时间阈值计算与分析向量-雅可比积（vector-Jacobian product），对极端规模采用概率分桶（probabilistic bracketing）仅排序核噪声分数的不确定中间带。

Lightweight method: 论文本身不提出模型轻量化方法，但其提出的可微软top-k算子可用于注意力剪枝（attention pruning）和稀疏路由，这属于模型加速/稀疏化范畴。但论文未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化方法在遥感或检测模型上的应用。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。仅报告了GPU上的处理速度：处理10^6、10^7、10^8个分数分别耗时0.41ms、1.15ms、5.23ms。未提及参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗、实时推理等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明标准遥感或目标检测数据集。实验在稀疏对抗样本生成和稀疏图像编码两个任务上验证，使用百万级坐标的稀疏预算设置。

Experiments: 在稀疏对抗样本生成任务上，相比SOTA方法实现一个数量级加速；在可微稀疏图像编码器训练上验证了端到端可微性。未与遥感目标检测方法对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未在目标检测任务上验证；3. 未讨论模型轻量化或星上部署；4. 仅报告GPU速度，未报告CPU/边缘设备延迟或能耗。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。可借鉴的点：Fast LapSum作为可微软top-k算子，理论上可用于遥感目标检测模型中的注意力剪枝（attention pruning）或稀疏特征选择，帮助减少计算量。但论文本身未做任何遥感或检测实验，需要自行验证其在遥感场景下的有效性。直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: The top-$k$ operation is a fundamental building block of modern sparse computation, enabling token routing, expert activation, memory selection, and attention pruning. Yet standard hard top-$k$ blocks gradients, while existing continuous (soft) relaxations remain too costly for large-scale models. We introduce Fast LapSum, an exact-budget soft top-$k$ primitive whose GPU solver runs in linear time after sorting. Unlike prior linear-time methods such as DFTopK, which relax the normalization constraint, Fast LapSum is, to our knowledge, the first method to preserve an exact selection mass of $k$ while remaining fully differentiable end-to-end. Our solver combines a linear-time threshold computation with an analytical vector--Jacobian product, and for extreme scales employs probabilistic bracketing to sort only the uncertain middle band of kernel-noised scores. The resulting overhead is almost negligible: the solver processes $10^6$, $10^7$, and $10^8$ scores in $0.41$, $1.15$, and $5.23$\,ms, respectively. This makes exact soft top-$k$ practical for sparse routing, retrieval, and large-scale optimization. We demonstrate Fast LapSum on two demanding applications operating over millions of coordinates inside the training loop: generating megapixel sparse adversarial examples with an exact soft budget of ${\sim}0.02\%$ of an image's pixels, achieving an order-of-magnitude speedup over state-of-the-art methods, and training a fully differentiable sparse image coder from scratch.

</details>


### [240] [ReGraph: Learning to Generate Recipe Graphs from Food Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06917)
*Guoshan Liu,Bin Zhu,Pengkun Jiao,Jingjing Chen,Chong-Wah Ngo,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReGraph食谱图数据集和RGL两阶段框架，用于从食物图像生成结构化烹饪流程图，提升大模型对烹饪过程知识的建模能力。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是食物图像到烹饪流程图的生成任务，属于多模态大模型在食品计算领域的应用，不涉及遥感、卫星、地球观测、目标检测或模型轻量化等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：食谱图生成（Recipe Graph Generation）；应用场景：食品计算、烹饪过程理解；传感器类型：普通食物图像（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：食材、烹饪动作、工具等实体。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于LMM（Large Multimodal Model）的两阶段框架RGL，具体骨干网络为两种代表性LMM（摘要未说明具体模型名称）。第一阶段生成过程分解，第二阶段生成结构化食谱图。摘要未说明网络具体结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等），也未涉及实时推理或边缘部署。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息，也未涉及星上或边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ReGraph（自建大规模食谱图数据集，含Recipe Reasoning Chain-of-Thought (RR-CoT) 追踪）；评价指标：文本生成指标（具体名称摘要未说明）以及基于schema匹配的结构化评估协议（实体和关系结构的对齐程度）。

Experiments: 实验发现：现有方法在文本生成指标上表现良好，但在ReGraph schema下可恢复的实体和关系结构有限；RGL在两种LMM骨干上均能提升烹饪实体和过程关系的生成质量；细粒度食材状态捕获仍是最具挑战的维度。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性信息。论文自身局限性：细粒度食材状态捕获仍具挑战。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但可注意以下两点：（1）论文使用图结构表示过程知识的方法，在遥感场景中可用于表示目标间的空间关系或时序关系；（2）Chain-of-Thought推理追踪的思路可用于遥感目标检测中的推理过程可解释性。但这些仅为间接启发，与轻量化核心目标无关。

Abstract: Recent Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have achieved impressive performance in recipe generation from food images.However, cooking is a structured transformation process in which ingredients undergo state changes through ordered actions,while free-form recipe language leaves the corresponding entities, intermediate states, and dependencies largely implicit and entangled.A graph representation makes this procedural knowledge explicit and compositional, providing a structured basis for assessing whether model outputs encode process-level knowledge rather than merely presenting plausible textual descriptions. To address this limitation, we present ReGraph, a large-scale recipe graph dataset that represents ingredients, cooking actions, and tools as entities, uses entity attributes to describe ingredient state changes, and employs typed relations to encode manipulation targets, destinations, and procedural ordering. ReGraph further incorporates explicit Recipe Reasoning Chain-of-Thought (RR-CoT) traces, providing auxiliary supervision for procedural decomposition and structured graph generation. Building on ReGraph, we propose Recipe Graph Learning (RGL), a two-stage framework that enables LMMs to generate a plausible fine-grained cooking workflow from a food image in the form of a structured recipe graph. Under a deterministic, schema-aware matching protocol, our experiments reveal a substantial gap between text-generation quality and recoverable procedural structure: recipes produced by existing approaches achieve competitive text-generation scores yet yield limited reference-aligned entity and relation structure under the ReGraph schema. In contrast, across two representative LMM backbones, RGL consistently improves the generation of cooking entities and procedural relations, while our analysis further shows that fine-grained ingredient-state capture remains the most challenging dimension.

</details>


### [241] [Deal Me Maybe: The Role of Emotions in Multi-Agent Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06922)
*Massimiliano Luca,Apoorva Singh,Bruno Lepri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Research relevance: Relevance analysis unavailable

Model architecture: Model architecture extraction unavailable

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Negotiation is a demanding social task for LLM agents, requiring strategic reasoning, persuasion, and interpersonal adaptation. Yet existing benchmarks often treat agents as emotionally neutral, overlooking a key driver of human bargaining behavior. We study how prompt-conditioned emotions affect LLM-based price negotiation. In a controlled framework, buyer and seller agents are independently assigned one of six emotional states and negotiate over 350 real consumer products under two budget conditions. Across 36 emotion-pair settings and five widely used LLMs, we find that emotions strongly shape outcomes. Angry buyers almost never reach agreement (0.39% deal rate), while happy buyers agree most often (28.91%), but obtain worse prices than fearful buyers. Emotion effects are role-dependent: buyer emotion mainly drives acceptance and rejection, whereas seller emotion shapes concession dynamics. These effects influence not only language, but also termination behavior and price trajectories, raising concerns for emotion-conditioned agents in commerce.

</details>

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到pipeline优化实现了speedup，但与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 四个不同的团队协作数据集。评价指标：团队绩效预测能力。具体数据集名称和指标摘要未说明。

Abstract: Designing autonomous agents that effectively assist human teams hinges on understanding team dynamics, often without task specific knowledge. We present TRIBE, a domain independent approach that reveals team behavioral dynamics invisible to traditional performance metrics. We show that communication patterns can categorize teams into performance predictive behavioral tribes, as early as 10% into the task, enabling timely interventions. We test TRIBE on four diverse datasets and demonstrate that communication patterns predict team performance while the prediction strength varies by the degree a task structure allows for behavioral freedom. Our temporal analysis reveals that AI agents significantly alter team behavioral trajectories while human advisors align with natural dynamics, and that teams maintain behavioral flexibility throughout collaboration. Further, we compare TRIBE to Llama and optimize the pipeline, achieving significant speedup with performance improvement.

</details>


### [243] [Science Edge Evaluation: SEE the Missing Step Toward Real Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06931)
*Taolin Han,Yuchen Zhang,Jinghang Wang,Yun Wu,Wai Yuet Chiu,Zhaohai Li,Yifei Zhang,Jinxin Wang,Yuhao Zhou,Chen Zhao,Jiajia Li,Jiaxin Li,Qile Jin,Kewei Sun,Shuang Wu,Weiqi Zhai,Renquan Lv,Junchao Li,Ruodan Chen,Qingteng Chen,Zhibo Yang,Hu Wei,Lin Qu,Shuai Bai,Bing Zhao*

Model architecture: 评估了19种多模态大语言模型（MLLMs），包括通用模型和科学专用模型，具体架构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: Science Edge Evaluation (SEE) 基准，包含专家策划的化学、生物、材料科学问题。评价指标为准确率（accuracy）。

Experiments: 评估19个MLLM，最佳模型准确率48.7%；使用工具后最佳准确率提升至52.7%。通用模型平均优于科学专用模型。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于大语言模型科学推理评估，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly involved in scientific discovery, yet it remains unclear whether they can support complex real laboratory science. Here we introduce Science Edge Evaluation (SEE), a multimodal benchmark of expert-curated questions grounded in peer-reviewed literature and experimental practice in chemistry, biology, and materials science. Evaluation of 19 multimodal large language models (MLLMs) shows that even the best-performing model reaches only 48.7% accuracy. Moreover, general-purpose models outperform science-specialized models on average. In the visual-agent evaluation, the use of tools increases the best accuracy to 52.7%. Tool use can expand the information available to models, but more information does not necessarily lead to reliable scientific reasoning. The key challenge is whether models can manage tool-derived information within the boundaries of the original experimental evidence. Together, these findings reveal that current MLLMs still cannot reliably make justified and evidence-bounded inferences from experimental results, which is an essential capability in real scientific discovery. Bridging this gap requires MLLMs to transition from explaining established scientific concepts to deriving novel and evidence-based insights from experimental data.

</details>


### [244] [Blind to the Pivotal Vote: Aggregate Independence Metrics Miss Where Verification Actually Helps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06940)
*Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM评估面板中裁判误差的相关性及通过测试套件信号进行单票替换的效用，属于大语言模型评估方法论，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化、在轨部署等任何相关主题，属于大语言模型(LLM)评估方法论研究，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为LLM评估面板的误差分析与信号替换策略，应用场景为代码基准测试(HumanEval+/MBPP+)，不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何视觉模型或目标检测网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何模型压缩/加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：HumanEval+/MBPP+（代码基准测试）。评价指标：准确率(accuracy)、有效投票数(effective-vote count)、边际决策效用(margin-stratified utility)。不涉及遥感数据集。

Experiments: 在HumanEval+/MBPP+上，多数侧替换规则将整体准确率从82.44%提升至85.62%，信号单独使用达到87.60%。涉及9裁判扩展和56个依赖子采样检查。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的视觉检测或模型压缩内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究LLM评估面板的统计误差分析，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: LLM judge panels are a standard evaluation tool, but prior work reports highly correlated panel errors: nine judges provide roughly the effective information of two independent ones, and aggregation closes only a small fraction of the gap. A natural remedy--a signal from a different evidence source, e.g., executing a test suite--produced no distinguishable change in the panel's effective-vote count at scale (-0.04, 95\% CI [-0.10, +0.02]). Aggregate dependence and conditional decision utility are different questions. Elementary majority arithmetic fixes the affected set for single-ballot substitution: only decisions with a one-vote margin can change. The empirical question is whether panel error rates rise and useful substitutions concentrate there. They do: the entire accuracy gain concentrates on these pivotal queries, where it is large (+10.4 to +23.3 percentage points across three headline configurations), and is exactly zero elsewhere. We confirm the pattern across three code benchmarks and four panel sizes (a 9-judge extension and 56 dependent subsampling checks, gain +6.5 to +16.1 percentage points). On HumanEval+/MBPP+, a majority-side replacement rule raises overall accuracy from 82.44\% to 85.62\% while invoking the signal on 16.2\% of queries; signal-only remains stronger at 87.60\%. Thus population-level dependence diagnostics and margin-stratified utility are complementary, and the affected-set characterization yields a call-reduction rule for any specified single-ballot substitution policy.

</details>


### [245] [LMM Modality Transfer: A Pre-requisite for Autonomous GIS Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06948)
*Ivan Majic,Zexian Huang,Franziska Hübl,Krzysztof Janowicz,Meilin Shi,Mina Karimi,Zilong Liu,Alexandra Fortacz-Lazan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究大型多模态模型（LMMs）在图像与文本模态之间转移空间信息的能力，通过"描述彩色方格图像→根据文本重新生成图像"的模态转移任务，发现当前LMMs（OpenAI）在简单空间网格的模态转移上仍表现不佳，指出实现强健地理空间理解需要严格的多模态对齐。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文涉及GIS/geospatial理解与空间推理，但核心是LMMs的模态转移能力评估，属于泛视觉-语言多模态机器学习研究。它不涉及遥感图像目标检测，也不涉及模型轻量化或星上部署。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向仅在地理空间理解这一宽泛主题上有间接关联，无直接技术借鉴。

Reading priority: 1分。论文聚焦LMMs模态转移能力评估，与遥感在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及目标检测、遥感影像或任何部署/压缩技术，仅作泛读了解即可。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：LMMs的模态转移能力评估（图像→文本→图像重建）。场景：GIS工作流自动化、空间推理。传感器类型：非遥感，为人工生成的彩色方格规则网格图像。目标类别：无目标检测，仅彩色方格空间场景。

Model architecture: 使用大型多模态模型（LMMs），具体为OpenAI的模型。架构细节（如具体模型名称、结构）摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（pruning, quantization, knowledge distillation, NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：人工生成的彩色方格规则网格图像（非公开遥感数据集）。评价指标：模态转移能力（图像重建的准确性/保真度），具体指标名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 实验：让一个LMM描述输入图像，再用新的LMM实例根据文本描述重新生成原始空间场景图像。结果表明OpenAI的LMMs在简单空间网格的模态转移上仍存在困难。

Limitations: 摘要未说明具体局限性。但可推断：仅测试了OpenAI模型、仅使用简单规则网格场景、未涉及真实遥感影像、未涉及目标检测或部署性能。与本研究方向的关联性极弱。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值有限。唯一可参考点是"多模态对齐"思想——若未来在轨遥感目标检测引入视觉-语言模型（如遥感图像描述辅助检测），需注意模态对齐的瓶颈。但该论文本身不提供任何轻量化或检测方法，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无可复现方向。

Abstract: AI models are becoming increasingly adept at understanding and processing spatial information, thereby facilitating agentic problem-solving in spatial tasks and workflows. However, most of the research on their spatial capabilities (e.g., spatial reasoning) has focused on the textual modality as input and output. This contrasts with the human approach to GIS workflows, where text and visual modalities are often used together, interchangeably, and in a complementary manner. Thus, to truly achieve an automated GIS analysis pipeline or carry out human-designed GIS workflows, AI models --- Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) in particular --- need to be able to seamlessly transition between image- and text-based modalities that are traditionally used in such workflows. We present a modality transfer task that (1) asks an LMM to first describe an input image of colored squares in a regular grid, and (2) asks a new LMM instance to re-generate an image of the original spatial scene using the textual description output by the former model. This task quantifies the ability of LMMs to transfer spatial information between image and text modalities. Ultimately, by examining the modality transfer capability of LMMs through the lens of spatial information theory, this work highlights a critical bottleneck: achieving strong and robust geospatial understanding in LMMs requires rigorous, multi-modal alignment. Our results indicate that recent LMMs (here from OpenAI) still struggle with modality transfer, when tasked with re-generating an image of a simple spatial grid of color squares.

</details>


### [246] [Does Splitting a Triage Decision Across Agents Hide Bias or Help Catch It? A Multi-Agent Simulation Study of LLM-Based Resource Allocation Under Audit Capacity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06949)
*Paul-Peter Arslan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究多智能体流水线（含审计步骤）中LLM决策的偏见问题，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何轻量化或部署技术。论文聚焦于LLM多智能体流水线中的偏见检测问题，属于AI伦理/安全领域，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM多智能体流水线中的偏见检测与审计；应用场景：灾难分诊资源分配决策；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 单智能体控制条件 vs 九智能体流水线（评估、分配、独立审计三个角色分工），使用GPT-4o-mini模型。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用合成灾难分诊模拟器，生成临床相同但人口统计属性不同的配对案例。共运行192个episode（2,304个已解决案例对）。评价指标：偏见发生率（biased outcomes rate）、审计捕获率（bias caught rate）、覆盖率（coverage rate）、判断准确率（judgment accuracy）。

Experiments: 单智能体条件偏见发生率6.9%，多智能体流水线6.1%（p=0.498，无显著差异）。审计容量对偏见捕获有显著影响：30.0%的偏见结果完全未被检测到，审计过载时升至43.8%，未过载时降至18.4%。覆盖率从100.0%降至65.6%（p<0.001）是主要驱动因素，而非判断质量下降（81.6% vs 85.7%, p=1.000）。按风险排序审计队列可恢复大部分覆盖率（65.6%→91.7%, p=0.028）。

Limitations: 作者自述局限性：仅使用一个模型（GPT-4o-mini）、样本量适中、未进行对抗性复制实验。此外，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术或方法。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无任何可借鉴点。不涉及遥感、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等任何相关技术。

Abstract: Prior benchmarking work has shown that a single large language model (LLM), forced to make life-or-death resource-allocation decisions, exhibits measurable demographic bias. Real deployments, however, rarely use a single agent: they use pipelines, with review steps meant to catch exactly this kind of failure. We study what happens to bias when the same decision is distributed across a role-differentiated multi-agent pipeline (assessment, allocation, independent audit) instead of made and checked by one model alone. Using a synthetic disaster-triage simulator with paired cases that are clinically identical except for one demographic attribute, we run 192 episodes (2,304 resolved case pairs) on GPT-4o-mini comparing a single-agent control condition to a nine-agent pipeline under three independently varied pressure dimensions. We find no measurable difference in how often biased outcomes occur between the two conditions (6.9% vs. 6.1%, p = 0.498). We do find a large and significant effect of audit capacity on whether bias is caught: 30.0% of biased outcomes go entirely undetected, rising to 43.8% when the auditor is overloaded and falling to 18.4% when it is not. Decomposing this effect shows it is driven almost entirely by coverage (whether a case is reviewed at all, which collapses from 100.0% to 65.6% under load, p < 0.001) rather than by degraded judgment on the cases that are reviewed (81.6% vs. 85.7%, p = 1.000, direction reversed). A follow-up experiment shows that reordering the audit queue by estimated risk, rather than first-come-first-served, recovers most of the lost coverage under the same capacity constraint (65.6% to 91.7%, p = 0.028). We discuss the implications for any system that adds independent oversight to an LLM agent pipeline under resource constraints, and report the study's limitations honestly: one model, modest sample sizes, and no adversarial replication.

</details>


### [247] [Critical Acclaim Orientation in Large Language Models: Evidence from Film Preference Elicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06955)
*Jonghyun Jee,Aaron Shaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究大型语言模型（LLMs）是否系统性地复现人类对电影的评价层级，通过8个模型、200部电影基准和Bradley-Terry估计，发现模型普遍偏向"影评界认可"而非"商业成功"的电影，且该倾向随模型规模增大而增强。

Research relevance: 与本研究方向基本无关（1分）。论文研究的是LLM对电影评价的文化偏见（critical acclaim vs commercial success），属于自然语言处理/文化社会学范畴，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能，也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测、遥感场景理解，更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何部署相关技术。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为LLM文化偏见研究，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：LLM对电影评价偏好的文化偏见分析（film evaluation / cultural bias）。场景：自然语言处理与计算社会科学，非遥感场景。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：电影（critically acclaimed, commercially successful, dual-legitimacy）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体网络结构。涉及8个模型，来自四个模型家族：Anthropic、OpenAI、Alibaba、Mistral。使用Bradley-Terry估计进行成对强制选择分析，以及嵌套OLS回归分析。模型规模（model scale）作为变量被考察。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未提及任何模型压缩或加速方法（pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS、轻量网络等均未出现）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。虽然提到"real-world LLM deployments"，但这是指LLM在实际应用中的部署，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：200部电影基准（200-film benchmark），分为三类：critically acclaimed（影评界认可）、commercially successful（商业成功）、dual-legitimacy（两者兼具）。每个模型进行20,000次成对强制选择比较。指标：Bradley-Terry估计、嵌套OLS回归。

Experiments: 主要结果：所有模型一致表现出"影评界认可"导向——影评界认可但商业上默默无闻的电影被优先于商业成功但影评界未认可的电影。该模式在每个模型家族内随模型规模增大而增强。调整public visibility后，模型对dual-legitimacy电影相对critical acclaim-only电影的偏好发生反转；进一步考虑popular reception后，商业成功电影的劣势被削弱。评价导向与推荐导向的提示框架产生不同排名。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，因此其局限性对本课题无参考价值。摘要本身未说明模型具体版本、训练数据细节、评价标准定义等。作为文化偏见研究，其结论仅适用于LLM文本评价场景。

Ideas for my research: 对本课题无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的观察是"模型规模增大导致行为模式增强"这一现象，但这与遥感目标检测轻量化方向无关。若强行联系，可类比为"模型规模与行为倾向的关系"，但无实际借鉴价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are trained on corpora that contain expressions of human judgment about films, books, music, and more. Yet whether LLMs systematically reproduce evaluative hierarchies remains unclear. Prior research on cultural bias in LLMs suggests competing expectations: models may mirror the popularity signals of internet texts, or may reproduce forms of prestige embedded in critical discourse. We probe this question through a study of film evaluations with eight models from four families (Anthropic, OpenAI, Alibaba, and Mistral), using a 200-film benchmark partitioned into critically acclaimed, commercially successful, and dual-legitimacy (critical acclaim + commercial success) films. Across 20,000 pairwise forced-choice comparisons per model analyzed with Bradley--Terry estimation, we observe a consistent critical acclaim orientation with all models: critically acclaimed yet commercially obscure films are selected over commercially successful yet critically unrecognized ones. This pattern grows with model scale within each family. In addition, nested OLS regression analyses show that evaluative orientation, public visibility, and popular reception distinctly help explain preferences. Adjusting for public visibility reverses the models' preference for dual-legitimacy films over critical acclaim-only films, while additionally accounting for popular reception attenuates much of the disadvantage of films with commercial success only. Finally, evaluative and recommendation-oriented prompt framings produce divergent rankings, suggesting that critical acclaim orientation may manifest indirectly in real-world LLM deployments.

</details>


### [248] [CAi Copilot: Reducing Operational Workload in Molecular Design through Intent-Driven Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06961)
*Zhu Wang,Jiangyu Chen,Yingjun Shang,Yuhui Yao,Laiao Lu,Tianfan Fu,Na Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CAi Copilot，一个面向早期分子设计的专家导向AI智能体，将研究意图转化为可执行、可追溯的分子设计工作流。

Research relevance: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文主题是分子设计（molecular design）工作流智能体，属于计算化学/药物设计领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能，也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测或遥感场景理解，更不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等轻量化与部署技术。属于泛AI智能体/工作流编排方法，与本研究方向弱相关。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为分子设计智能体，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，无直接技术借鉴价值，仅可作为泛AI工作流编排的间接参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：分子设计工作流执行（intent-to-evidence molecular design workflow execution），属于AI智能体（agent）编排与多工具协调。应用场景：早期分子设计（early-stage molecular design）、药物/化合物设计。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感传感器）。目标类别：分子候选物（molecular candidates），非遥感目标。

Model architecture: CAi Copilot智能体，包含三个链接层：Research Interface Layer（将意图转化为可执行计划）、Agent Reasoning Layer（利用中间结果引导每次运行）、Execution Substrate（提供分子工具、指标、可复用工具和后端服务）。属于多智能体/工作流编排架构，非神经网络检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及模型压缩或加速方法，无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署，无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：45个任务（45 tasks）的基准测试。评价指标：outcome score（结果得分），CAi达到84.59，超过次优结果18.07分。另有基准测试评估生成、筛选、多准则评价的协调能力。未涉及遥感数据集。

Experiments: 在45个任务上CAi取得最强综合性能，outcome score为84.59，比次优结果高18.07分。额外基准测试评估了CAi在生成、筛选、多准则评价上的协调能力，同时暴露了其在长时程执行（long-horizon execution）上的局限。

Limitations: 摘要明确指出在长时程执行（long-horizon execution）上存在局限。此外，论文与遥感、目标检测、轻量化完全无关，无法为在轨目标检测提供任何直接技术借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。唯一可能间接相关的点是其"intent-to-evidence"工作流编排思想——即把复杂研究意图拆解为可执行、可追溯的步骤，并利用中间结果自适应调整。这一思想或可类比到"在轨目标检测模型轻量化"的自动化流程编排（如自动化的剪枝-量化-蒸馏流水线调度），但属于方法论层面的泛化启发，并非直接技术方案。总体而言，对遥感在轨检测与轻量化课题的直接可借鉴点很少。

Abstract: Early-stage molecular design is an iterative process, not just a task of generating molecules. Researchers turn broad goals into design strategies, refine candidates, assess many properties, and gather evidence before synthesis and tests. AI methods can generate molecules, optimize several goals, predict properties, dock compounds, and account for synthesis. Yet these functions are spread across specialized tools. Experts must still coordinate each step, judge interim results, and integrate evidence. The central challenge is thus to turn research intent into adaptive, traceable runs grounded in scientific tools. We cast this challenge as intent-to-evidence molecular design workflow execution and present CAi Copilot, an expert-oriented agent with three linked layers. The Research Interface Layer turns intent into an executable plan. The Agent Reasoning Layer uses interim results to guide each run. The Execution Substrate supplies molecular tools, metrics, reusable utilities, and backend services. Across 45 tasks, CAi achieves the strongest overall performance, with an outcome score of 84.59, exceeding the next-best result by 18.07 points. Additional benchmarks test how CAi coordinates generation, screening, and multi-criteria evaluation, while exposing limits in long-horizon execution. These results show that CAi turns broad molecular-design intent into transparent, traceable workflows that connect interim decisions to candidate-level evidence.

</details>


### [249] [Learning in Deep Networks under Dale's Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06963)
*Roy Abel,Shimon Ullman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种符合Dale约束的生物可塑性神经网络架构，通过两个互补的非负通道（on-off表示）表示正负贡献，结合局部Hebbian学习规则，在仅使用非负误差信号的情况下理论上可精确恢复backpropagation更新，并在Tiny ImageNet上取得优于普通网络的性能。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于生物可塑性学习/神经科学计算建模方向，与遥感、卫星、在轨部署、目标检测均无直接关联。它不涉及遥感图像、目标检测任务，也不涉及模型压缩或边缘部署。唯一可能的间接借鉴点是其"非负激活+固定符号突触"的约束思想与量化/二值化网络（binary/ternary networks）在"限制权重符号与取值"上有一定概念相似性，但论文本身并未面向部署或检测场景。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，属于生物可塑性学习/神经科学计算建模。仅对量化/符号约束网络设计有极间接的启发，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：生物可塑性神经网络学习（biologically plausible learning）。应用场景：神经科学计算建模、通用图像分类。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感）。目标类别：Tiny ImageNet通用图像分类，非遥感目标。摘要未说明遥感/卫星/目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 采用两个互补交互的非负通道（on-off representations）分别表示正负贡献，通过一个简单神经回路motif在网络的bottom-up和top-down通路中重复实现。神经元激活和学习信号均为非负，突触符号固定（满足Dale's constraint）。结合局部Hebbian学习规则，仅通过神经元间局部交互传播学习信号并更新权重。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及传统轻量化方法（pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS等）。但架构本身具有"非负激活+固定符号突触"的约束特性，与二值/三值网络（binary/ternary networks）在权重符号约束上有概念相似性，可能隐含计算/存储效率优势，但摘要未明确说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟等部署指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。虽然"局部Hebbian学习+非负表示"理论上可能带来计算简化，但论文未做任何边缘/星上部署评估。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Tiny ImageNet benchmark。评价指标：分类准确率（相对vanilla网络的性能增益）。摘要未说明其他数据集或指标。

Experiments: 理论证明：在仅依赖非负误差信号的情况下，学习方案可精确恢复backpropagation更新。实验：on-off架构在Tiny ImageNet上相比vanilla网络取得substantial gains，同时满足更强的生物约束。摘要未说明消融实验、对比方法细节。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化部署完全无关，属于神经科学计算建模方向。2. 仅在Tiny ImageNet上验证，未在遥感数据集（如DOTA、NWPU VHR-10、SAR舰船等）上测试。3. 未提供任何部署指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗）。4. 摘要未说明该方法在目标检测任务上的适用性。5. 生物可塑性约束带来的计算开销/训练复杂度未讨论。

Ideas for my research: 1. 该论文的"非负激活+固定符号突触"约束与二值/三值量化网络在权重符号限制上有概念相通之处，可借鉴其on-off双通道表示思想，探索在遥感目标检测骨干网络中引入符号约束的轻量化设计。2. 局部Hebbian学习规则可减少全局梯度传播依赖，对星上低功耗、低通信开销的在线学习/自适应微调可能有启发。3. 但整体相关性弱，建议仅作为量化/符号约束设计的灵感来源，不宜作为核心参考。

Abstract: Biologically plausible learning models aim to explain how neural circuits can implement effective learning under the constraints of real neurons. Although significant progress has been made, a major remaining challenge is that existing models often allow neurons or synapses to represent mixed-sign values, both positive and negative, in violation of a basic aspect of cortical circuitry -- Dale's constraint: biological neurons are either excitatory or inhibitory, but not both, and synapses cannot change sign. In this work, we address this discrepancy by introducing a biologically motivated neural architecture in which both neural activations and learning signals are represented by non-negative activity, and synapses have fixed sign, while still supporting backpropagation-like learning. Our approach uses two complementary interacting non-negative channels to represent positive and negative contributions, inspired by evidence of on-off representations in the brain. These channels are implemented through a simple neural circuit motif, which is repeated throughout the network in both bottom-up and top-down pathways. Combined with a local Hebbian learning rule, the resulting model propagates learning signals and updates weights using only local interactions between neurons. We show theoretically that our learning scheme can exactly recover the backpropagation update despite relying solely on non-negative error signals. Empirically, beyond satisfying stronger biological constraints, the on-off architecture learns efficient representations, yielding substantial gains over comparable vanilla networks on the Tiny ImageNet benchmark. These results demonstrate that effective learning can emerge from biologically plausible mechanisms without requiring mixed-sign signals, providing a step toward more realistic models of neural computation.

</details>


### [250] [Finding Usable Weight Mechanisms with Tiled SVD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06969)
*Ash Manvi,Samreena Tajreen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种从线性层直接提取"机制挂载点"(mechanism mounts)的机械可解释性方法，通过列分块SVD将每个挂载点表示为(触发,写入,强度)三元组，并在Gemma-2-2B上验证其能有效识别网络权重中的概念身份。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。该论文属于大语言模型(LLM)的机械可解释性(mechanistic interpretability)研究，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关系。它既不涉及遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及模型压缩或部署。唯一可能的间接关联是：其通过SVD分解线性层权重来理解网络内部机制的方法，理论上可迁移到任何神经网络（包括遥感检测模型）的权重分析，但摘要中完全没有涉及遥感或检测场景。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文是LLM机械可解释性研究，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及遥感场景、目标检测任务或任何模型压缩/部署技术。仅当对SVD权重分析用于剪枝通道重要性评估感兴趣时，可作为方法学参考，但优先级很低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：大语言模型机械可解释性（mechanistic interpretability），即从网络权重中提取可解释的机制挂载点。场景：LLM内部机制分析，非遥感场景。传感器类型：不适用（文本数据WikiText-2）。目标类别：LLM中的概念(concepts)，非遥感目标。

Model architecture: 方法核心是column-tiled SVD（列分块奇异值分解），从线性层（linear sites）直接提取机制挂载点，每个挂载点是一个三元组(v,u,σ)，分别表示trigger（触发）、write（写入）、strength（强度）。评估对象为Gemma-2-2B的七个线性映射：mlp.down、attn.o、mlp.gate、attn.q、attn.k、mlp.up、attn.v。与主流方法（训练sparse autoencoder代理字典）不同，该方法直接从网络权重本身提取身份信息。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。摘要未提及剪枝(pruning)、量化(quantization)、知识蒸馏(knowledge distillation)、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何模型压缩或加速技术。SVD分解在此处是用于可解释性分析而非模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文完全不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。实验对象Gemma-2-2B是LLM，与在轨遥感部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：WikiText-2（16,384-token子样本）。模型：Gemma-2-2B。评价指标：采用预注册(pre-registered)的测试套件，以full-write energy lift（全写入能量提升）而非tile-local lift作为评判标准。结果：residual writes（mlp.down、attn.o）获得full A/B/C评分，通过52/52个site-layers；其他映射（mlp.gate/attn.q/attn.k/mlp.up/attn.v）仅获A/B评分，各26/26。总计182/182 GO。

Experiments: 在Gemma-2-2B上对七个线性映射进行评分。residual writes（mlp.down、attn.o）表现最佳，获得full A/B/C评分并通过全部52个site-layers；其余五个映射仅获A/B评分。所有182个测试点全部通过(GO)。作者还发布了库代码、语料构建器、实验入口和单元测试。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但可推断：1) 该方法仅在大语言模型上验证，未在视觉/遥感模型上测试；2) 评估基于文本语料WikiText-2，与遥感图像数据无关；3) 未涉及任何部署或轻量化考量；4) 方法有效性依赖"full-write energy lift"这一特定评判标准，其普适性未知。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限但存在间接价值：1) column-tiled SVD从权重本身提取可解释结构的思想，理论上可迁移到遥感检测骨干网络（如YOLO、Faster R-CNN的卷积/线性层）的权重分析，帮助理解检测模型内部哪些通道/权重承载关键特征，从而指导结构化剪枝（pruning）——识别"低strength"的挂载点可视为冗余权重，辅助轻量化；2) 其"从权重规则而非代理字典提取身份"的思路，与无需训练即可评估通道重要性的剪枝方法（如基于SVD的通道剪枝）有相通之处；3) 但需注意，该论文是LLM可解释性工作，与遥感目标检测轻量化课题相关性很弱，仅可作为权重分析方法的灵感来源。

Abstract: The dominant approach to mechanistic interpretability trains proxy dictionaries such as sparse autoencoders and labels features from max-activating text. The best such atlases identify con- cepts, but that identity lives in the learned dictionary rather than in the network weights them- selves. We propose extracting mechanism mounts directly from linear sites by column-tiled SVD: each mount is a triple (v,u,σ) read as trigger, write, and strength. Identity is the weight rule.
